## BUG-233 - verify-release secrets-scan crashes on vanished/broken tree entries (FileNotFoundError from Path.rglob walk)

- **Severity**: P2 (release-gate robustness; verify-release must always produce a verdict, never an unhandled crash) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/release/verify.py::_secrets_scan
- **Root Cause**: per-file p.is_file() and p.stat() were unguarded; any OSError escaping the loop crashed verify_release BEFORE any verdict was emitted (fail-crash instead of fail-safe).
- **Fix**: is_file() and stat() are each wrapped in try/except OSError -> skip the entry and continue. Decode path already had (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) handling; the size guard and lstat path now share the same discipline. Scan semantics unchanged for all normally-readable files.
- **Verification**: py_compile, ruff check+format, mypy src clean; new regression test green; the original probe (398s scan over 1.1GB worktree tree) now completes with a FAIL verdict carrying the synthetic test-credential hits instead of crashing.
- **Regression Test**: tests/unit/test_release_system.py::test_secrets_scan_survives_unreadable_entries (new; monkeypatched Path.is_file raising OSError for one entry -> scan completes, verdict emitted, other files still scanned).
- **Symptom**: kout): `nexus verify-release --root <tree>` raised FileNotFoundError (WinError 3) mid-walk when the scanned tree contained a reparse/broken nested path (observed: .worktrees/subagent-*/.worktrees/subagent-*/... path that vanished between rglob enumeration and stat). rglob() enumerates lazily; entries can disappear or become unreadable between enumeration and p.is_file()/p.stat().
- **Note**: not this fix): the same scan is a wall-time hazard when --root points at a huge tree (measured 398s on a 1.1GB local tree). Release CI only ever scans the portable bundle (small). Consider a size cap or ignore-list for the CLI default if operator-facing scans of dev trees become common.
- **Tests**: test_release_system; test_secrets_scan_survives_unreadable_entries; tests/unit/test_release_system.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/release/verify.py; src/nexus_scalp/release/verify.py
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8058

## BUG-234 — TRAIN vs LIVE HTF window asymmetry (MLPWR-06-02): feat_41 (h1_momentum) and feat_42 (m30_structure) structurally 0.0 in every training row vs real …

- **Severity**: P0 (training/inference feature divergence — same class as BUG-190/197B/217) | STATUS: FIXED (2026-09-04 ~00:45, MLFIX-T1 feature-layer lane; verified by parity probes + regression suite; dataset regeneration + champion retrain still REQUIRED downstream — P0 pre-retrain blocker cleared)
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/model_generation/schema_v2.py::compute_70d_frame` (and `compute_60d_frame`), `src/nexus_scalp/features/scalp_features.py::ScalpFeatureEngine.compute_from_bars`
- **Symptom**: Full-vector parity corpus is bit-exact on 68/70 features (`PARITY VERDICT: MISMATCH`, max_delta 3.0, TOL 1e-12) — mismatches ONLY at idx 41 `htf_h1_momentum` (train 0.0 / live 3.0) and idx 42 `htf_m30_structure` (train 0.0 / live 1.0). In live serving, 2,206 signals on 2026-09-03 were decided on a feature regime the model never saw during training; scaler std on feat_42 is 0.001 (training-variance floor) so live values saturate immediately to clip limit.
- **Note**: BUG-235 (retrain persist guard) is a SEPARATE defect (different surface: `live_engine._trigger_async_online_fine_tune`); it is owned by the persist-path lane and NOT fixed by this feature-layer change.
- **Root Cause (Proven)**: `ScalpFeatureEngine.compute_from_bars` slices the last 55 bars for base features (`:526 tail_bars = completed_bars[-55:]`) but aggregates HTF (`:741-744`) from the FULL `completed_bars` list passed in. The TRAIN builder `schema_v2.py:611` passed `window = all_bars[max(0, i-54):i+1]` (always 55 bars), yielding ≤1 H1 bucket (needs ≥2, i.e. ≥120 bars) and ≤1 M30 bucket (needs ≥5, i.e. ≥150 bars) — making both features structurally 0.0 across the entire training history. The LIVE caller `live_engine.py:3618` passes the aggregator's full depth (~900 to 4000 bars), producing real nonzero values.
- **Fix**: MLFix §3 option-b variant — CALLER-DEPTH alignment on the shared contract): added `HTF_HISTORY_BARS = 4000` as the SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH in `features/scalp_features.py` (the feature layer owns the semantic, no feature-name/order change, schema hash `235b8fccc96b7e0e` PRESERVED). Rationale vs alternatives: (a) window-normalizing HTF inside `compute_from_bars` (option c) would silently change values computed with <4000 bars of history and would re-write the engine contract mid-incident with a retrain already queued — rejected; (b) passing a live-equivalent depth to the train builder (option b, chosen) is the smallest correct layer: the live aggregator cap is ALREADY 4000 (`live_engine.py:3618`, post-BUG-058 resync seeds 3500), so the only divergent caller was the TRAIN builder (and the replay path `replay_70d_vector` which sliced `base_window = bars[-min_bars:]` = 55). Live-side wiring required: NONE — `live_engine.py` cap 4000 == `HTF_HISTORY_BARS` by construction (documented in code comment; the constant is the contract the engine cap already implements).
- **Changed Files**: `features/scalp_features.py` (+`HTF_HISTORY_BARS=4000` shared contract), `model_generation/schema_v2.py` …
- **Tests**: test_70d_bug106_incremental_phase19; test_70d_replay_parity_task3; test_htf_train_live_parity; test_liquidity_engine_causality; tests/unit/test_htf_train_live_parity.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/features/scalp_features.py; nexus_scalp/model_generation/schema_v2.py; src/nexus_scalp/features/scalp_features.py; src/nexus_scalp/model_generation/schema_v2.py
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8070

## BUG-235 — LiveEngine async retrain unconditionally persists baseline weights on zero-improvement / quality-gate rejection (self-perpetuating degenerate …

- **Severity**: P1 (model integrity self-perpetuation) | STATUS: FIX_IN_PROGRESS
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/application/live_engine.py::_trigger_async_online_fine_tune`
- **Symptom**: When online fine-tuning fails the quality gate or produces zero improvement, `WalkForwardTrainer.fine_tune_online` returns the unchanged baseline model (after logging BUG-228 honest skip or rejection). However, `_trigger_async_online_fine_tune` unconditionally called `self._save_model_weights_atomic(updated_model, bundle.artifact_path)` and `self._register_active_model(..., replaced=True)`. This updated the on-disk mtime (e.g. 20:44:00 tonight), re-persisted identical/degenerate weights, and logged `ASYNC RETRAIN SUCCESS` — misreporting a rejected training pass as a successful deployment and masking artifact stagnation.
- **Fix**: `WalkForwardTrainer.fine_tune_online` now returns a structured result or `LiveEngine` compares `_state_dicts_equal(updated_model.state_dict(), bundle.model.state_dict())` (or inspects an `accepted` flag); if the model is unchanged / rejected, the atomic disk write and provenance replacement are SKIPPED, logging `ASYNC RETRAIN SKIPPED (baseline kept)` instead of `ASYNC RETRAIN SUCCESS`.
- **Root Cause (Proven)**: `_trigger_async_online_fine_tune` did not inspect whether the returned model was accepted or merely the reverted baseline before calling the atomic save and provenance re-registration.
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/application/live_engine.py; src/nexus_scalp/application/live_engine.py
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8083

## BUG-236 - Strategy Marketplace, Isolated Research DB & Adaptive Runtime

- **Severity**: P0 FEATURE (marketplace, research DB isolation, 14-factor scoring, 13 pack generators, runtime snapshot store, BUG-233 short-circuit) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/marketplace/* (13 packs, scoring.py/measurement.py/repair.py/snapshot.py/service.py/store.py), src/nexus_scalp/research/pipeline.py, src/nexus_scalp/web/api_v1/marketplace.py, src/nexus_scalp/web/api_v1_wiring.py, Web/marketplace.js, Web/index.html, Web/app.js, tests/unit/*
- **Tests**: tests/unit/test_research_gate_shortcircuit_bug233.py (2 regression, red-before/green-after), tests/unit/test_marketplace_backend.py (12), tests/unit/test_web_marketplace_contract.py (8) — 24 passing RC=0. Ruff (+format) pass, py_compile pass.
- **What**: Built the controlled Strategy Marketplace + Research Lab + Live Strategy Runtime foundation. Proven: gate short-circuit was wasting 3766 robustness evaluations on OOS-failed runs — fix saves full robustness pass + evidence write on every OOS-failed run (REJECTED semantics unchanged). Marketplace gives 13 deterministic pack generators (N configurable) producing valid DSL-only seeds, isolated marketplace.db, 14-factor explainable scoring model with honest NOT_AVAILABLE for missing tiers, evolution-backed repair, hot-reload runtime-snapshot contract attuned to runtime_config.py, 12-route versioned API under /api/v1/marketplace, and a 6-panel Dashboard tab wired through window.NX.api. ## BUG-237 - `nexus update` FAILED "artifact is not a valid zip" on the Inno setup.exe payload

- **Severity**: HIGH (update flow broken for every setup-install user) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/release/updater.py (UpdateOrchestrator._verify_payload_manifest, UpdatePlanBuilder._select_asset)
- **Fix**: (1) _verify_payload_manifest returns immediately for -setup.exe payloads — the Inno installer embeds SHA256SUMS.txt + release-manifest.json itself (BUG-166 pre-stage); its integrity gate is the artifact SHA-256, already checked. (2) _select_asset now prefers the PORTABLE ZIP over the setup.exe — the zip installs headless on every mode and carries build-info.json + release-manifest.json for full verification; setup.exe remains the fallback when no zip is published. No gate weakened: zip payloads still fully manifest-verify (including bad-manifest BLOCK), corrupt zips still fail with the same clear message.
- **Verification**: py_compile clean; ruff check+format clean; mypy updater.py clean; test_release_system.py 27 passed; test_release_hardening.py 15 passed/1 env-skip; new suite 6/6.
- **Regression Tests**: tests/unit/test_bug237_update_setup_payload.py (6): zip preferred over setup; setup fallback when no zip; setup.exe skip; zip-without-manifest passes; zip with WRONG embedded manifest still BLOCKS; corrupt zip still "not a valid zip". End-to-end probe on a fixture release (both assets + digest metadata): plan picks the zip, both verify paths behave.
- **Symptom**: PowerShell `nexus update` on the venv CLI): Downloading -> Verifying -> "Update finished / Status: FAILED / Error: artifact is not a valid zip". update-history.jsonl row FAILED from 9.0.6 -> 9.0.7; update-state.json FAILED_SAFE; SHA-256 had PASSED (cache NexusScalpEngine-9.0.7-win-x64-setup.exe hash 133b1d63... == SHA256SUMS.txt).
- **Root Cause (Proven)**: _verify_payload_manifest() opened EVERY downloaded artifact with zipfile.ZipFile to check the embedded release-manifest. The GitHub release publishes TWO payloads; _select_asset() preferred the -setup.exe, so setup-install users downloaded the INNO EXE (magic MZ, not PK), BadZipFile fired, and a perfectly verified artifact was reported FAILED. Verified live: repo releases v9.0.5-9.0.7 all publish setup.exe BEFORE the zip in asset order; cached setup.exe is byte-valid per SHA256SUMS.
- **Tests**: test_bug237_update_setup_payload; test_release_hardening; test_release_system; tests/unit/test_bug237_update_setup_payload.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/release/updater.py; src/nexus_scalp/release/updater.py
- **Commits**: 133b1d63
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8120

## BUG-238 - shadow promotion_verdict noise-band branch raised NameError: VERDICT_INSUFFICIENT (lazy-import aliasing missed one branch)

- **Symptom**: tests/unit/test_shadow_replay_evidence_chg0047.py 2 red (TestInvalidPairExcluded::test_invalid_pairs_counted_but_never_scored, TestDeterminism::test_same_inputs_identical_evidence); runtime evidence build crashed with NameError: name 'VERDICT_INSUFFICIENT' is not defined at src/nexus_scalp/shadow/_replay_evidence.py:269 exactly when a shadow replay evidence run resolved >= min_resolved_pairs but landed in the noise band (the honest INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE verdict path for small paired samples).
- **Root Cause**: commit 37ae4d31 (CodeQL py/unsafe-cyclic-import batch) converted the module-level `from nexus_scalp.shadow.replay import VERDICT_*` to lazy aliased imports (_VI/_VR/_VS) and aliased 3 of the 4 return branches; the final noise-band fallback return kept the bare module-level name, which no longer exists at runtime (TYPE_CHECKING-only). Path-unexercised defect: the three aliased branches mask the fourth in casual runs; only the noise-band branch (small resolved-pair counts) hits it.
- **Fix**: line 269 `"verdict": _VI,` — same aliased constant as the other branches; byte-safe single-anchor edit, py_compile clean, probe-verified before (NameError reproduced with pairs_resolved=50, mean=-0.005, median=0.001) and after (verdict INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE returned).
- **Tests**: tests/unit/test_shadow_replay_evidence_chg0047.py full file re-run GREEN after fix (was 2 FAILED before). Class lesson (extends BUG-182A): a lazy-import refactor that aliases constants must grep EVERY use site of each moved name in the same file; mypy catches undefined names only if the branch is type-reachable, not if it is rarely executed.
- **Tests**: test_invalid_pairs_counted_but_never_scored; test_same_inputs_identical_evidence; test_shadow_replay_evidence_chg0047; tests/unit/test_shadow_replay_evidence_chg0047.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/shadow/_replay_evidence.py; src/nexus_scalp/shadow/_replay_evidence.py
- **Commits**: 37ae4d31
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8128

## BUG-245 - OOSGate empty context-contract match silently widened the gate to the global population (false-PASS class)

- **Severity**: HIGH (validation integrity — OOS hard gate could PASS on a population the strategy never declared) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/research/oos.py OOSGate.evaluate (empty-match branch) | CHG-0063.
- **Symptom**: a candidate whose declared context contract matched ZERO samples got an OOS verdict computed on the FULL dataset. Reproduced: 100 London-negative samples (session=LONDON, expectancy -0.4R) + 20 Asian-positive OOS tail (session=ASIAN, +0.9R) + typo contract sessions=['LONDONN'] -> OOSGate PASS +0.73R on the WRONG population (the pipeline-level CONTEXT_CONTRACT_EMPTY_POPULATION raise never fired because the gate was called directly, e.g. orchestrator._evaluate_oos / operator tools / tests).
- **Root Cause**: the pre-PHASE-27 defensive branch `if filtered: ... else: context_diag['sufficient_evidence']=False` left dataset_for_eval as the FULL dataset and continued into split/evaluate — a silent population widening. The pipeline path was hardened earlier (fail-loud at pipeline.py:239) but the GATE itself kept the fallback, so any direct caller bypassed the fail-loud.
- **Fix**: empty-match branch now logs [OOS] event=CONTEXT_CONTRACT_EMPTY_POPULATION and returns an explicit OOSResult FAIL (oos_samples=0, reason CONTEXT_CONTRACT_EMPTY_POPULATION, matched_samples=0, sufficient_evidence=False). Matching-contract and no-contract paths are byte-identical (no threshold change, INV-008 untouched).
- **Tests**: tests/unit/test_agent17_oos_hard_gate.py (9 tests, xdist-safe) — empty-contract FAIL red-before/green-after, matching-contract PASS preserved, temporal split determinism/disjoint/chronology, purge of boundary-crossing horizon, strict train<oos ordering, zero-OOS FAIL, negative-OOS FAIL, degradation ceiling, NaN-R exclusion. Combined suite with phase09b + purge_defaults_bug183 + forward_test_freeze + bug233 short-circuit: 68 passed.
- **Commit**: 390e153d (fix + tests, pushed origin/main) + d8ed39ad (ruff format follow-up).
- **Tests**: test_agent17_oos_hard_gate; tests/unit/test_agent17_oos_hard_gate.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/research/oos.py; src/nexus_scalp/research/oos.py

## BUG-239 - RiskEngine.evaluate_proposal crashed with UnboundLocalError on the micro-account + insufficient-margin path AND the trailing micro-account exception …

- **Symptom**: equity<50 proposal with margin_free ~0 raised `UnboundLocalError: cannot access local variable 'slippage_usd'` at the success log (risk_engine.py ~line 522) instead of returning None; the same tail re-applied `symbol_info.volume_min` AFTER the free-margin guard had zeroed the volume, i.e. the micro exception bypassed the margin check entirely (crash masked the resurrection in most paths).
- **Root Cause**: slippage_usd only bound inside the Almgren-Chriss impact while-loop; when the loop never runs (volume below broker minimum) the success log still references it. The micro-exception reapply had no margin re-verification.
- **Evidence**: FAIL-BEFORE probe reproduced the crash exactly (equity=40, margin_free=0.001); PASS-AFTER returns None; micro+margin-ok still returns 0.01. Commit 152e8ebe.
- **Fix**: slippage_usd initialized to 0.0 before the loop; micro exception only rescues an impact-reduced positive volume and re-verifies the rescue keeps required margin <= margin_free; a volume zeroed by the margin/exposure guards stays zeroed (fail-closed, structured MICRO_ACCOUNT_*_REJECTED reasons).
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_agent11_execution_risk_forensic.py TestBug239MicroAccountMarginHole (4 tests).
- **Invariants**: INV-003 preserved/strengthened (risk authoritative, fail-closed).
- **Tests**: test_agent11_execution_risk_forensic; tests/unit/test_agent11_execution_risk_forensic.py
- **Invariants**: INV-003

## BUG-242 - Web operator close/modify endpoints bypassed OrderLifecycleManager

- **Symptom**: POST /api/positions/close and /api/positions/modify called engine.adapter.* directly - the only broker-mutation surface outside the manager (repo-wide call-site census). No audit order row, no exit mechanism (autopsy attribution gap), no cache release, no SAFE_MODE / SHADOW boundary checks on the mutation.
- **Fix**: OrderLifecycleManager.close_position_manual(ticket) tags ExitMechanism.MANUAL_CLOSE BEFORE the broker call, writes an audit log_order row (execution_mode=MANUAL) on success, releases the ticket from the live tickets cache, rolls the mechanism tag back on refusal; modify_position_manual mirrors this with a MANUAL audit row. Web routes now resolve engine.order_manager and call the wrappers (manager unavailable -> HTTP 400 fail-closed). Commit d0a9b6d4.
- **Regression**: TestBug242ManualActionAuthority (4 tests, incl. black-box TestClient probe asserting the adapter is never called directly).
- **Invariants**: INV-004 now holds for 100% of reachable broker-mutation call sites; INV-012 preserved (MANUAL_CLOSE is evidence-backed).

## BUG-243 - Champion registry: stale direct-CHAMPION row `t70d_v1_full`

- **Symptom**: `t70d_v1_full` (scalp_v3/70D on the live bundle, fingerprint `c8c0b5b06d4c094d`) was created via direct `set_status(CHAMPION)` outside the governed `VERIFY -> APPROVED -> CHAMPION` promotion transaction. `ModelLifecycleRegistry.champion()` returned the foreign id `t70d_v1_full` as production champion, masking the governed identity `primary_scalp_scalp_v1_50d`.
- **Root Cause**: `_sync_champion_registry_state` compared ONLY `artifact_path`; a foreign row on the same path with a contradictory `(schema_id, dimension)` contract was treated as `already_truthful` (NOOP).
- **Fix**: Added `LiveEngine._evaluate_champion_registry_sync()` as the pure contract-aware decision (verifies full `(path, schema_id, dimension)` triple). `_sync_champion_registry_state` ROUTES that decision: `REPAIR -> CHAMPION->ARCHIVED` demotion (append-only, never deleted) BEFORE re-stamping the truthful serving row. Immediate DB remediation: row `id=4015` `t70d_v1_full` moved `CHAMPION->ARCHIVED` with appended `REGISTRY_REPAIRED` event.
- **Tests**: `tests/unit/test_agent3_champion_registry_sync.py` (3, red-before/green-after); ruff/mypy/py_compile clean.
- **Tests**: test_agent3_champion_registry_sync; tests/unit/test_agent3_champion_registry_sync.py

## BUG-244 - bar-mode replay phantom TP for SELL: `ev.low <= TP >= ev.low` degenerates to `TP >= low`

- **Severity**: CRITICAL (bar-mode backtest/replay phantom exits) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/research/streaming_replay.py:900 (StreamingReplayEngine._process_event bar branch, SELL TP containment)
- **Impact**: every SELL with TP above any subsequent bar's high before TP was reached exited prematurely at TP with fabricated PnL (e.g. gap-down bar [2600,2610] AFTER entry @2652.5 with TP=2640: bar never reached 2640, but phantom TP exit recorded +50 USD; profit_factor / expectancy / win_rate / drawdown all fabricated).
- **Root Cause**: the SELL TP check used the Python chained comparison `ev.low <= state.open_pos.take_profit >= ev.low`, which operator-precedence collapses to `take_profit >= ev.low`. For a SELL with TP above a bar's high, the check still returned True (TP >= low), firing a phantom TP exit at a level the bar never reached. Mirror case BUY never diverged (it correctly used `ev.high >= TP >= ev.low`).
- **Fix**: `ev.low <= take_profit <= ev.high` — the exact mirror of the BUY branch (symmetric containment). Single comparison operator, zero other behavior.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_agent15_bug244_bar_sell_tp_containment.py (4 tests): 1. gap-down phantom SELL TP above bar high — NO phantom exit post-fix (RED->GREEN: phantom present pre-fix), 2. SL-first tie-break intact for SELL (both levels inside range -> SL wins at SL level), 3. BUY branch regression guard (BUY SL containment unchanged), 4. deterministic ledger_hash guard (same bars + same run_id -> identical ledger_hash).
- **Commit**: 3f5bef2d (single-line operator correction) +Regression landed by Agent 15; parallel Agent-18 LIMIT pending-queue work (CHG-0062) developed concurrently on the same file but fixes a disjoint invariant.
- **Risk/Handoff**: none for execution — bar-mode replay is a research-only path. Live dispatch untouched. Agent-18's LIMIT pending-queue hardening (CHG-0062) and any other 3f5bef2d+ research changes should keep this containment fix; ensure `take_profit <= ev.high` is not regressed by the absorption carrier (this fix survived 2 overwrites; committed atomically).
- **Tests**: test_agent15_bug244_bar_sell_tp_containment; tests/unit/test_agent15_bug244_bar_sell_tp_containment.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/research/streaming_replay.py; src/nexus_scalp/research/streaming_replay.py
- **Commits**: 3f5bef2d
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8237

## BUG-246 - dataset builder MAX_GAP_US drifted from the canonical temporal SSoT

- **Severity**: LOW (no impact on the authoritative artifact - proven; silent two-value contract risk) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `scripts/dev/regen_70d_clean_dataset.py` (builds authoritative `ds_70d_clean_m1_20260904`) vs `src/nexus_scalp/model_generation/temporal_contract.py` (`CANONICAL_MAX_GAP_US = 10*60*1_000_000 = 600_000_000`, SSoT per ARCH-SEQ-UNIFY).
- **Symptom**: regen script hard-coded `MAX_GAP_US = 15*60*1_000_000 = 900_000_000` while the temporal-contract SSoT declares 600_000_000; the dataset manifest recorded `contract.temporal_max_gap_us = 900000000` (and `sequence_windows.max_gap_us = 900000000` in verification.json) while every trained artifact's `model.meta.json` stamps `max_gap_us = 600000000` via `WalkForwardTrainer._save_metadata` - a silent two-value window contract across dataset-vs-model.
- **Fix**: `regen_70d_clean_dataset.py` now imports `CANONICAL_MAX_GAP_US` from the SSoT and sets `MAX_GAP_US = CANONICAL_MAX_GAP_US` - one canonical source, no local copy to drift.
- **Verification**: py_compile clean; ruff check+format clean; `test_a2_data_lineage_bounded` 1/1 PASS (`-p no:cacheprovider`).
- **Regression**: `tests/unit/test_a2_data_lineage_bounded.py` (wired into `tests/critical_suite.txt`): deterministic 5k-bar M1 tail build via the canonical `build_70d_dataset` path validating the full lineage RAW -> CLEAN -> FEATURES -> LABELS -> DATASET -> MODEL INPUT (~15 s): OHLCV integrity, timestamp monotonicity, 70D hash/ordering (`235b8fccc96b7e0e`), clipping |x|<=3.0, BUG-234 feat_41/42 non-zero pin, UTC-aware timestamps, 3-class label hygiene, eval/purge overlap 0, lineage CLEAN_HISTORICAL, scaler-family std census, and `SequenceBuilder` gap-safe windows built with the SSoT constant.
- **Note**: Agent-3's BUG-243 registry row and Agent-11's BUG-239..242 execution rows are different classes; this is the dataset lane's only ledger claim. Evidence probes: `scratch/ns_a2_perturbation_probe{,_out.json}`, `scratch/ns_a2_lineage_probe.py`.
- **Impact On The Authoritative Artifact**: NONE (proven, not assumed). Gap census on `ds_70d_clean_m1_20260904` (probe `scratch/ns_a2_perturbation_probe_out.json` -> `gap_census`): deltas >600s = 76, >900s = 76, in (600s, 900s] = 0 - the 300s band is empty on the 100k M1 history, so window validity counts are identical under either constant and the parquet is byte-identical. `SequenceBuilder` itself already imports the SSoT at HEAD; only the script's local literal lagged.
- **Tests**: test_a2_data_lineage_bounded; tests/unit/test_a2_data_lineage_bounded.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/model_generation/temporal_contract.py; src/nexus_scalp/model_generation/temporal_contract.py

## BUG-239 (dataset-identity class) - replay-cache dataset could be silently rebuilt under the SAME id with DIFFERENT bytes; read path served …

- **Severity**: P0 (dataset trust chain: "what exact bytes produced this result"
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/research/mt5_tick_dataset.py (acquire_ticks cache check + acquire_bars identity + load()/event_source() read path) - PROBES (Temp/agent14_probes/, venv python, synthetic adapters on the probed get_tick_history/get_rate_history surface; results captured in-session): 1) acquire_bars dataset_id embedded datetime.now(UTC): the same historical start re-acquired later minted a NEW id (probe: d403ce4a... vs bb6dabbe...), so cache never hit and the manifest carried no end. 2) parquet-without-meta (interrupted/lost manifest) + re-acquire: parquet OVERWRITTEN with the new content under the SAME dataset_id and the fingerprint silently changed => one identity, two different byte sets over time (corrections did NOT create a new dataset). 3) read path had zero integrity verification: in-place byte mutation loaded silently; appended fake row loaded silently; a swapped foreign dataset (EURUSD parquet under the XAUUSD id) was served; a manifest record-count mismatch was ignored. 4) a dead adapter (0 rows for EVERY chunk) published + permanently cached a records=0 dataset with a VALID fingerprint and no failure marker; a partial outage (data then empty chunk) cached with no marker — incomplete acquisition could masquerade as complete. 5) adapter rows outside [start,end) were stored silently (BUG-188-class timebase recurrence had no defense-in-depth gate). 6) hostile symbols (../, a/b, C:, XAU/USD, empty) reached the id/file path ('..' produced a cache_root-parent write); racing identical acquisitions crashed on tmp.replace (WinError 2/32) and produced duplicated rows. 7) provenance: git_commit="" serialized as "" instead of NOT_RECORDED.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_agent14_dataset_integrity.py (19 tests, RED on the pre-fix tree @7715e4a8, GREEN on the hardened module).
- **Note**: parallel agents independently claimed BUG-239/BUG-240 for other surfaces (Agent-11 risk_engine BUG-239 @152e8ebe; Agent-6 web toggle BUG-239/BUG-240 @CHG-0060/0064). This is a THIRD distinct defect in the same ID window — dataset-layer row per the bugs.md duplicate-ID disambiguation rule (document here as BUG-239-DATASET; do not rewrite the other rows).
- **Fix**: _tick_dataset.py v3, CHG-0061): explicit `end` for acquire_bars (id = symbol|kind|start|end; wall-clock identity preserved for legacy no-end callers via meta_version=1); orphan-parquet re-acquire RAISES ArtifactConflict (IMMUTABLE ARTIFACT CONFLICT) — corrections must mint a new id; load()/event_source() recompute the content fingerprint and check manifest coherence, raising DatasetCorruptionError on any tamper/swap/ append/count mismatch …
- **Tests**: test_agent14_dataset_integrity; tests/unit/test_agent14_dataset_integrity.py

## BUG-247 - Execution pipeline residual hedge/reversal entry gates

- **Severity**: MEDIUM (risk-boundary integrity on two remaining entry paths; no production bypass at HEAD after this fix) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py:execute_order` (hedge entry, P2) + `src/nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py:_run_protection_chain` AI-flip fast reversal (P1)
- **Root Cause**: two entry paths inside the same manager had weaker gate discipline than the primary `dispatch_order` path — the AI-flip direct-pending path was added for latency (placed after the protective close with no circuit hop) and the hedge direct-order path reused the pre-clamped RiskEngine volume without a last-defense clamp.
- **Evidence**: P2 live paper probe before/after: `TradeOrder(volume=100) -> execute_order` before fix volume=100.0 (bypass), after fix volume=10.0 (hard cap). P1 probe: SAFE_MODE reversal suppresses the pending while closing the position. Regression tests `tests/unit/test_agent12_bug247_hardening.py` (3, offline PaperMT5Adapter): huge volume clamped + zero-clamp refused + reversal SAFE_MODE gate; `tests/unit/test_agent12_execution_forensic.py` 18/18 still green. ruff check+format + mypy + py_compile clean on order_manager.py.
- **Regression**: `tests/unit/test_agent12_bug247_hardening.py:TestBug247HedgeHardCap::test_direct_100_is_clamped_to_10`, `::test_clamped_zero_is_refused`, `::test_source_gate_present` (all green; idempotency guard already covered in the main battery).
- **Invariants**: INV-003/INV-004 strengthened (risk/clamp authority now on all three entry paths: dispatch, hedge, flip). No thresholds changed. No live MT5 smoke (safety contract).
- **Symptom**: parallel subagents, 2026-09-05): every broker-write call-site census proved single-authority (all outside calls inside OrderManager), but inside the manager two entry legs bypassed the primary-path risk gates: (P1) `order_manager.py:5257` `adapter.place_pending_order(...)` inside the AI-direction-flip fast-reversal chain bypassed the `SAFE_MODE` circuit (only a `HARD_MAX_LOTS` clamp ran). The protective CLOSE leg correctly fires in SAFE_MODE (risk-reducing), but the follow-up pending entry is itself an entry and must honor the circuit. Previously `execute_order:686` + `dispatch_order:836` both gated on SAFE_MODE while this direct pending did not. (P2) `order_manager.py:703` `adapter.send_order(order)` inside `execute_order` (the hedge entry path called from `live_engine.py:_evaluate_hedging_policy:4806`) bypassed the unconditional `HARD_MAX_LOTS=10.0` clamp (`order_manager.py:77/808`). The normal hedge flow calls `RiskEngine.evaluate_proposal -> get_clamped_position_size` which clamps, but a direct `TradeOrder(volume=100)` would bypass the 10-lot ceiling.
- **Fix**: same ordering as dispatch_order: SAFE_MODE -> exposure -> clamp): (P1) the fast-reversal follow-up now checks `if …
- **Tests**: test_agent12_bug247_hardening; test_agent12_execution_forensic; test_clamped_zero_is_refused; test_direct_100_is_clamped_to_10; test_source_gate_present; tests/unit/test_agent12_bug247_hardening.py; tests/unit/test_agent12_execution_forensic.py

## BUG-248 - walk-forward per-fold gate ignored the fold's own OOS window; walk_forward_folds still defaulted purge/embargo to 0.0

- **Root Cause**: (1) WalkForwardEngine.validate computed fold status from VALIDATION expectancy only, never comparing the oos_bt it had just computed; the only OOS protection was the aggregate avg_oos >= 0, which positive validation windows can carry. (2) walk_forward_folds kept 0.0 leakage-guard defaults while every production consumer passed the BUG-140 constants explicitly - a direct-call/default-reliance path still leaked.
- **Tests**: tests/unit/test_research_walkforward_gate_agent5.py (4: defaults==SSOT constants, default purge removes boundary-crossing horizon, negative-OOS fold never PASS, healthy folds still pass) - wired into tests/critical_suite.txt after test_research_purge_defaults_bug183.py.
- **Symptom (Db-Evidenced)**: all three lifecycle=VALIDATED registry rows carried folds whose OWN out-of-sample window was NEGATIVE yet stamped status=PASS (SF-D8F60509E1 fold1 oos=-0.130344 PASS; SF-0A58246C4D fold2 oos=-0.005886 PASS; SF-2A4D2DE80C fold3 oos=-0.073233 PASS). Scoring honors walkforward.passed, so a VALIDATED verdict could rest on folds whose own OOS lost money. Separately, splitting.walk_forward_folds (the shared fold producer under WalkForwardEngine.validate) still declared embargo_seconds=0.0 / purge_seconds=0.0 defaults - the BUG-183 class surviving one layer deeper than the 11ea316 fix (the four production gates were wired; the library-level producer was not).
- **Fix (Commit B1Eb5B94)**: walkforward.py per-fold status requires BOTH val_pass (val_expectancy_r > MIN_FOLD_EXPECTANCY_R) AND oos_pass (oos_expectancy_r >= MIN_FOLD_EXPECTANCY_R) before pass_count increments; splitting.py walk_forward_folds defaults route through the SSOT DEFAULT_PURGE_SECONDS/DEFAULT_EMBARGO_SECONDS. Byte-safe single-anchor edits, py_compile clean.
- **Reproducer**: synthetic 120-sample dataset, validation windows positive, fold-3-only OOS window -1.2R -> OLD: fold3 status=PASS, wf.passed=True (avg_oos +0.27); NEW: fold3 status=FAIL. Real-DB probe confirmed the negative-OOS-PASS pattern on all 3 VALIDATED rows before the fix (probe saved %LOCALAPPDATA%/Temp/agent5_forensic/probe_wf_fold_gate.py).
- **Adjacent Fix (Same Commit)**: the P0 emission gate (4c4b007e) aborted smoke drills on dataset provenance/schema/seq identity AFTER all folds + final training, killing TEST-70D-MODEL-31 (the BUG-103 head-contract regression origin). Gate provenance/schema/seq identity checks are now scoped to non-smoke (_is_smoke) publications; smoke quarantine (smoke=True -> production_eligible=False) unchanged and still enforced; non-smoke provenance stays HARD.
- **Scope Note**: Agent 17 (TASK-AGENT17-OOS-GATE, CHG-0063) owns the broader OOS/temporal-holdout mission and already landed BUG-245 (empty-context silent widening). The …
- **Tests**: test_research_purge_defaults_bug183; test_research_walkforward_gate_agent5; tests/unit/test_research_walkforward_gate_agent5.py
- **Commits**: 11ea316, 4c4b007e, b1eb5b94
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8325

## BUG-249 - Research methodology fail-open trio: negative purge/embargo silently disabled leakage guards + all-non-finite OOS window PASSED the hard gate + …

- **Severity**: P1 (research integrity / INV-008 evidence chain) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/research/splitting.py (split_temporal, walk_forward_folds), src/nexus_scalp/research/oos.py (OOSGate.evaluate), src/nexus_scalp/research/pipeline.py (validate_candidate provenance forwarding)
- **Verification**: py_compile clean on 3 src files; focused research suites green (agent13 7/7 + purge_defaults + walkforward gate + no_future_leakage); byte-verified after 4 parallel-swarm revert cycles.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_agent13_methodology_integrity.py (7 tests, green post 69033c54): negative purge raises / negative embargo raises / walk_forward negative raises / zero still allowed / all-NaN OOS FAILs / partial-NaN PASSes / run config records effective values.
- **Invariants**: INV-008 strengthened; no thresholds changed; append-only ledger row.
- **D1**: .py): purge_seconds/embargo_seconds gated only by `> 0`, so NEGATIVE values behaved exactly like 0.0 - a caller bug or config-parse error SILENTLY DISABLED the BUG-140/BUG-183 leakage guards (repro: split_temporal(ds, purge_seconds=-50) == split_temporal(ds, purge_seconds=0)). FIX: ValueError on purge/embargo < 0 in BOTH functions; explicit 0.0 opt-out preserved.
- **D2**: .py): compute_backtest maps all-non-finite R to total_trades=0 + expectancy 0.0; OOSGate checked oos_samples==0 but not oos_bt.total_trades, so a window holding SAMPLES but zero FINITE R rows PASSED the hard gate on fabricated 0.0 (repro: 8 finite + 4 all-NaN OOS -> PASS). FIX: fail-closed with oos_had_finite = oos_bt.total_trades > 0.
- **D3**: .py): validate_candidate forwarded purge/embargo to WalkForwardEngine/OOSGate but the STATIC_VALIDATION early _record_run, BACKTEST-empty _record_run, backtest.run(use_split=True) and terminal verdict _record_run used module defaults - research_runs.config recorded 300/60 regardless of effective run. FIX: forwards to all four sites; after-repro records 0.0/0.0.
- **Tests**: test_agent13_methodology_integrity; tests/unit/test_agent13_methodology_integrity.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/research/oos.py; nexus_scalp/research/pipeline.py; nexus_scalp/research/splitting.py; src/nexus_scalp/research/oos.py; src/nexus_scalp/research/pipeline.py; src/nexus_scalp/research/splitting.py
- **Invariants**: INV-008

## BUG-249-A6 (execution clamp NaN/inf leak; ID SUFFIX to avoid collision with the two BUG-249-class research rows) - OrderLifecycleManager._clamp_dispatch_volume …

- **Severity**: HIGH (unexplainable order divergence; INV-004 audit surface) | STATUS: FIXED (60b1f42f on main)
- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py OrderLifecycleManager._clamp_dispatch_volume (primary dispatch volume clamp, called by dispatch_order AND execute_order hedge path)
- **Fix**: two math.isfinite guards - (1) top of _clamp_dispatch_volume together with the vol<=0 check; (2) defense-in-depth AFTER the risk-engine clamp call (misbehaving clamp cannot leak NaN/inf). Both coerce to 0.0 -> dispatch_order already treats 0 as LOT_SIZE_REJECTED with terminal NOT_DISPATCHED outcome (BUG-140 path) - the divergence now fails loud with a ledger row.
- **Note**: BUG-248 ID was already consumed (Agent 5 research-gate renumber; Agent 13 BUG-249). This row uses the suffix BUG-249-A6 per the collision rule. No history rewritten.
- **Root Cause (Harness-Proven)**: float(volume) accepted NaN/inf; the only guard was vol <= 0.0, which is False for NaN; min(NaN, HARD_MAX_LOTS) is NaN and round(NaN,2) is NaN. A NaN volume could therefore reach execute_market_order/place_pending_order as the request volume - a broker request no audit row could explain (silent divergence class). Second vector: a misbehaving RiskEngine.get_clamped_position_size returning NaN/inf re-entered the same unguarded path after the clamp call.
- **Verification**: no MT5): nan clamp -> 0.0; inf clamp -> 0.0; misbehaving-clamp NaN -> 0.0 post-clamp; ruff check+format clean; mypy clean; py_compile clean; scoped execution suites green (pending_recovery, exit_bugs, execution_quality, BUG-212/232/226 paper boundary).
- **Wave-2 Full Harness (Agent 6)**: Q1 MT5 mapping matrix 13/13 (BUY/SELL/LIMIT/STOP x send_order + execute_market_order + place_pending_order + ambiguous-fill reuse + hard-reject single-send); Q2 risk edges 19/19 (NaN/inf/zero refusals, exact 1%-risk math, 20% margin clamp, micro-account exception, kill switch, max-concurrent); Q3/Q4 14/14 (duplicate request_id idempotency, MAX_TOTAL_EXPOSURE post-manage-tick, SAFE_MODE on dispatch+hedge, pending-state UNKNOWN semantics, BUG-244 phantom containment, gateway parity caf1c2cc). Harnesses in %LOCALAPPDATA%/Temp/agent6_w2_*.py (Temp, not repo - scratch hygiene).
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py; src/nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py
- **Invariants**: INV-004
- **Commits**: 60b1f42f, caf1c2cc
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8347

## BUG-250 - Exit-classification false-promoted UNKNOWN to MANUAL_CLOSE on bare deal_reason==0 when profit!=0

- **Severity**: MEDIUM (evidence provenance - INV-012 UNKNOWN must never become MANUAL on PnL alone) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/experience/outcome_recovery.py:classify_exit_with_evidence` - the `reason==0` branch (MT5 DEAL_REASON_CLIENT, ambiguous - 0 means both "client close" and "missing reason")
- **Root Cause**: the four-part `or` disjunction conflated "some broker evidence is present" with "the deal has PnL" - a price-based signal leaked into a reason-code taxonomy.
- **Evidence**: live probe before/after: `reason==0, profit=-12.34, comment="", sl=0` before fix `MANUAL_CLOSE/0.8`, after fix `UNKNOWN/0.2`; with `comment="client close"` or `exit_price` within `0.15` of `sl_price` still `MANUAL_CLOSE`. Situated regression `tests/unit/test_agent12_bug250_exit_classification.py` (3, offline): profit-alone stays UNKNOWN, comment/geometry promotion preserved. Broad accounting suites `test_trade_lifecycle_task3` + `test_performance_metric_truth` + `test_agent11_scenario_coverage_contract` stay green (102 passed).
- **Symptom**: t/accounting sweep): the `reason==0` promotion condition `if comment_l or near_sl or near_tp or profit != 0.0:` promoted a bare `deal_reason==0` with NO comment and NO price-geometry corroboration to `MANUAL_CLOSE/BROKER_DEAL_REASON/0.8` whenever `profit != 0.0` - which is *virtually always* (any filled close has PnL). A close whose only evidence is PnL magnitude is not a MANUAL_CLOSE; the real MT5 proof would be a broker comment (`mobile`/`web`) or exit-price proximity to the current SL/TP.
- **Fix**: 1-line predicate tighten): the condition is now `if comment_l or near_sl or near_tp:` (comment or geometry required; profit alone no longer promotes). The else-branch stays `UNKNOWN/FALLBACK_HEURISTIC/0.2` with the same docstring. Previously-promoted cases that had a client comment or near-SL/TP geometry still classify `MANUAL_CLOSE` unchanged.
- **Tests**: test_agent11_scenario_coverage_contract; test_agent12_bug250_exit_classification; test_performance_metric_truth; test_trade_lifecycle_task3; tests/unit/test_agent12_bug250_exit_classification.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/experience/outcome_recovery.py; src/nexus_scalp/experience/outcome_recovery.py
- **Invariants**: INV-012
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8357

## BUG-249 - Spread-gate parameter `max_spread_atr_ratio` was plumbed into the constructor but NEVER enforced

- **Severity**: MEDIUM (spread-risk guard advertised but not enforced: an ATR-normalized cap meant to fail-closed entries when the broker take dominates the scalp window was unreachable; only RiskEngine max_spread_points and regime HIGH_SPREAD_CHOP protected the path) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/signals/policy.py` (compute + wiring)
- **Symptom**: `SignalPolicy.__init__(max_spread_atr_ratio=0.18)` stored the limit but NO code path ever consulted it; a candidate with spread 0.90 (ratio 0.60 vs ATR 1.5, 3.3x the cap) still proceeded past every signal gate. The documented ATR-normalized protection was a dead parameter.
- **Root Cause**: the ATR-normalized guard never existed (pre-dated only by RiskEngine point-based max_spread_points and the tick-velocity/OFI regime classifier - neither ATR-normalized).
- **Fix**: wire the guard fail-closed in the signal policy: candidate `spread_atr_ratio = current_spread/atr` vs `max_spread_atr_ratio` with decision_stage=SPREAD_ATR_GATE / blocked_by=SPREAD_ATR_RATIO and reason SPREAD_ATR_RATIO_EXCEEDED (e.g. `0.60 > 0.18`) + evidence stamped into risk_checks (`spread_atr_ratio=0.6, max_spread_atr_ratio=0.18`). Zero-spread degenerate path never blocked by this gate.
- **Regression**: `tests/unit/test_agent5_bug249_251_252.py` (TestBug249SpreadAtrGate 3 tests, xdist-safe).
- **Invariants**: INV-018 respected (new risk_checks fields additive, observability only); no threshold value changed (0.18 default kept).
- **Evidence**: repo venv): narrow spread 0.20 vs ATR 1.5 (ratio 0.133) passes to FINAL_DECISION; wide spread 0.90 (ratio 0.60) blocks at SPREAD_ATR_GATE with reason SPREAD_ATR_RATIO_EXCEEDED; ruff clean, mypy clean, 3/3 regression tests in tests/unit/test_agent5_bug249_251_252.py::TestBug249SpreadAtrGate.
- **Tests**: test_agent5_bug249_251_252; tests/unit/test_agent5_bug249_251_252.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/signals/policy.py; src/nexus_scalp/signals/policy.py
- **Invariants**: INV-018
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8368

## BUG-251 - AI-reversal close/flip gate consumed RAW probabilities while the entry confidence gate is trained-class normalized

- **Severity**: MEDIUM (decision-consistency defect: reversal and entry gates measured the same model output on two different scales; could both over-fire and under-fire flips vs what the calibrated threshold means) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/signals/policy.py::_evaluate_ai_reversal` (bias computation + confidence stamp on the CLOSE_POSITION proposal)
- **Symptom**: since CHG-0042 the entry confidence gate measures `max(buy,sell)/(buy+sell+no_trade)` (trained-class normalization, WAIT excluded). The AI-reversal gate still computed relative bias as `buy/(buy+sell)` over RAW directional probs and stamped the RAW prob as proposal confidence. With a large NO_TRADE mass (the production serving head is near-uniform, ~0.3-0.35 per class) a conviction that PASSES the entry gate could FAIL the reversal bias (or vice versa): e.g. sell=0.30/buy=0.20/no_trade=0.40 -> entry-scale sell confidence 0.333 but raw-only bias 0.60 (would pass rel_threshold 0.60); sell=0.55/buy=0.10/no_trade=0.35 -> raw bias 0.846 passes, trained bias 0.550 would fail the same threshold. The close-then-flip protocol therefore disagreed with the confidence semantics the rest of the pipeline is calibrated on.
- **Root Cause**: the 2026-09-02 CHG-0042 confidence-semantics repair normalized the entry gate but did not propagate to `_evaluate_ai_reversal`, which kept its pre-repair raw-probability arithmetic (and no `no_trade_prob` input at all).
- **Regression**: `tests/unit/test_agent5_bug249_251_252.py::TestBug251ReversalConfidenceSemantics` (3: large-NO_TRADE suppression, genuine-conviction normalized stamp 0.733, degenerate-mass fallback no-crash).
- **Invariants**: INV-018 respected (evidence fields only); no threshold changed; CHG-0042 semantics now uniform across entry AND reversal gates.
- **Fix**: s, semantics-aligned on the scale): `_evaluate_ai_reversal` now takes `no_trade_prob` (passed from the call site sanitized `prob_no_trade`), normalizes the relative bias over the trained mass `max(0,buy)+max(0,sell)+max(0,no_trade)` (degenerate mass falls back to the raw directional share, never manufactures conviction), and stamps the reversal CLOSE_POSITION proposal confidence in the SAME trained-class scale (clamp >= 0, no manufacturing). Thresholds (ai_flip_relative_bias_threshold 0.60, ai_flip_min_delta 0.10) and structural-confirmation requirements UNCHANGED.
- **Evidence**: repo venv): sell=0.55/buy=0.10/no_trade=0.10 -> bias 0.733 >= 0.60 fires with confidence 0.733 (was raw 0.55); sell=0.30/buy=0.20/no_trade=0.40 -> trained bias 0.333 < 0.60 and delta 0.10 NOT > 0.10 -> NO false reversal (old raw bias 0.60 >= 0.60 would have fired). ruff clean, mypy clean, py_compile clean.
- **Tests**: test_agent5_bug249_251_252; tests/unit/test_agent5_bug249_251_252.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/signals/policy.py; src/nexus_scalp/signals/policy.py
- **Invariants**: INV-018
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8380

## BUG-253 - 70D inference permanently blocked by stale liquidity snapshot: tick_pipeline duplicate aggregator.process_tick clobbered is_new_bar, killing the …

- **Severity**: CRITICAL (70D champion served but inference blocked on every tick: '70D inference requested but liquidity snapshot is not VALID (stale/missing)' raised ~12.5k times/day; entries dead while positions still managed; every failure fed the hot-path consecutive-error circuit breaker toward survival mode) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/application/live/tick_pipeline.py::run_pre_policy_stages` (duplicate `self.om.aggregator.process_tick(tick)`; liquidity re-warm trigger; two inference call sites)
- **Regression**: scratch/bug253_harness.py (4-case behavior harness; keep as evidence). The 8 warmup-gate unit reds are the known-foreign throttle-timestamp bug (taskboard/memory), not this fix.
- **Invariants**: INV-001 (no DB on hot path - unchanged), INV-008 (causality - snapshot stays VALID-or-blocked, never fabricated), INV-018 (degraded state observable via existing INFERENCE_BLOCKED_70D_ASSEMBLY telemetry + INFO re-warm logs), INV-020 (liquidity information-only), BUG-169 (no per-tick liquidity compute; retry throttled 15s).
- **Symptom**: 15:25): `[INFERENCE] 70D assembly failed - inference blocked for this tick` twice per tick (once in-trade via manage-active-positions, once in the policy slot), interleaved with REGIME TRANSITION + FRESHNESS_GATE BLOCKED_BY_STALE lines. Liquidity snapshot decision_at 15:39 vs feature timestamp 15:45 (age 6+ min > 4x90s causal window -> STALE), yet FEATURE_CALCULATION_OK at 19:09 and 21:17 proves the compute itself was healthy - the RE-WARM TRIGGER never fired between those runs.
- **Root Cause (Chain)**: (1) the L14 seam extraction left `aggregator.process_tick(tick)` in BOTH live_engine.py:2881 (caller) and tick_pipeline.py:374 (called seam). The caller advanced the forming bar, so the seam second call for the SAME tick always returned None and OVERWROTE the caller authoritative `is_new_bar` parameter -> `_on_new_bar` (radar/retrain records/MSLIE) never fired on bar close and `_warm_liquidity_from_bars` never re-warmed on new bars. (2) The retained snapshot aged past `LIVE_STALE_AFTER_SEC*4` -> `causal_state()=="STALE"` -> `build_live_feature_vector` raises (by design, no fabrication). (3) `run_pre_policy_stages` had NO degraded-state gate: the STALE raise propagated every tick instead of degrading to probs=None.
- **Fix**: quidity is NEVER fabricated): (a) removed the duplicate process_tick; caller is_new_bar is authoritative (documented in-place). (b) BUG-169 latency contract preserved: re-warm stays new-bar-cadence; added a THROTTLED (15s) retry ONLY when the retained snapshot is already STALE so a transient compute failure cannot strand the governor permanently. (c) both inference call sites in run_pre_policy_stages gate on `effective_feature_dim==70 -> liquidity_governor.causal_state()=="VALID"` (_liq_ok): degraded states yield probs=None (positions still managed, protective stops never pause) instead of raising; 50D contracts are untouched; genuine model defects still propagate and feed the circuit breaker (fail-loud …
- **Tests**: test_bug125; test_fault_injection; test_fidelity; test_htf_warmup_gate; test_runtime_70d_warmup
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/application/live/tick_pipeline.py; src/nexus_scalp/application/live/tick_pipeline.py
- **Invariants**: INV-001, INV-008, INV-018, INV-020
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8391

## BUG-252 - RiskEngine drawdown penalty was unreachable dead code (AccountInfo has no peak_equity field)

- **Severity**: MEDIUM (documented drawdown-aware position sizing never executed: during sustained drawdown the engine kept full risk-per-trade instead of the intended up-to-80% cut; risk-multiplier error on the live sizing path) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/risk/risk_engine.py::evaluate_proposal` (PART 7 drawdown scaling), `src/nexus_scalp/application/live_engine.py` (hedge-path call site)
- **Symptom**: `peak_equity = getattr(account, "peak_equity", account.equity)` ALWAYS resolved to `account.equity` because the frozen `AccountInfo` domain model (domain/models.py) has NO `peak_equity` field - the getattr default never fired and peak==equity on every call -> drawdown_pct == 0 -> the `max(0.2, 1-drawdown*0.2)` penalty was unreachable on every live path. The authoritative peak lives in `LiveEngine._peak_equity` (restored from the audit DB at startup, withdrawal-adjusted, used for survival mode / max-DD halt) but was never forwarded to the risk engine.
- **Root Cause**: contract seam - the risk engine expected an account field that the domain model never carried; nothing wired the engine's peak tracker into sizing.
- **Fix**: `RiskEngine.evaluate_proposal` gains `peak_equity: float | None = None` (precedence: explicit kwarg > account attr if a future model adds it > honest fallback account.equity; bool/NaN/inf/<=0 rejected -> fallback, never fabricated) and the LiveEngine hedge path forwards `peak_equity=getattr(self, "_peak_equity", None)`. No threshold changed (penalty formula and 1.0% DD trigger preserved).
- **Regression**: `tests/unit/test_agent5_bug249_251_252.py::TestBug252PeakEquityDrawdownPenalty` (2: no-peak honest fallback, 33% DD -> volume < 0.35x baseline).
- **Note**: numbered BUG-252 to avoid the BUG-250 ID collision with the Agent-12 exit-classification row already in the ledger.
- **Invariants**: INV-003 strengthened (risk engine now actually enforces its documented drawdown scaling); INV-018 (peak forwarded as data, not a decision mutation).
- **Tests**: test_agent5_bug249_251_252; tests/unit/test_agent5_bug249_251_252.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/application/live_engine.py; nexus_scalp/risk/risk_engine.py; src/nexus_scalp/application/live_engine.py; src/nexus_scalp/risk/risk_engine.py
- **Invariants**: INV-003, INV-018
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8401

## BUG-254 - Gate-first fresh install poisoned the audit DB: baseline skeletons shadowed the app DDL → ~553k dead-letter rows / 1GB in 15h

- **Severity**: CRITICAL (every audit INSERT failed for 15h at ~10 rows/s: 553,206 audit_dead_letter rows, audit.db 1,006MB; all decision/telemetry/lifecycle persistence lost while failing closed) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/database/engine.py::_create_baseline_tables` (skeleton heal), `src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/{audit_repository,dead_letter_store}.py` (worker salvage + dead-letter), `src/nexus_scalp/database/manifest.py` (AUDIT_SCHEMA_VERSION 8→9 alignment after 31cc2003 skipped it), NEW `src/nexus_scalp/database/app_columns.py` (APP_REQUIRED_COLUMNS contract)
- **Symptom**: dead-letter error corpus (probe, read-only): 440,470x "table audit_signals has no column named request_id", 93,065x "audit_guard_telemetry no window_start", 16,929x "position_lifecycle_events no event_key", 1,484x "strategy_registry no strategy_id", 784x/312x/154x/6x/1x/1x account_snapshots/experiences/orders/model_registry/worker_states; ALL error_type=OperationalError, ALL payload_note="audit worker batch-retry failure"
- **Fix**: (a) NEW database/app_columns.py — stdlib-only APP_REQUIRED_COLUMNS mapping every audit table to the columns its real INSERTs use (verified against the app DDL union); (b) _create_baseline_tables heals skeletons from it (PRAGMA-gated, additive-only, fail-loud, event=BASELINE_SKELETON_HEALED, slim-context ImportError fallback); (c) manifest AUDIT_SCHEMA_VERSION 8→9 (SSOT drift from 31cc2003); (d) dead-letter bounded retention (BUG-255). Regression: tests/unit/test_perf_deadletter_skeleton_repro.py (RED at HEAD incl. `no such column: order_id` bootstrap crash, GREEN after).
- **Invariants**: INV-023 (retention bounded, off tick path), DB-MIGRATION contract (heal is additive/idempotent/fail-loud), INV-001 (no hot-path change)
- **Root Cause**: each link verified live on the container): (1) docker entrypoint runs `nexus db migrate` BEFORE the engine; (2) on a fresh volume the migration engine's baseline builder creates every manifest table as an id-only skeleton (manifest declares those tables with zero columns); (3) the app bootstrap is CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS (no-op on skeletons) and its _add_column_if_missing loops heal only EXTRAS (execution_mode, reason_code, spread_usd...), never core columns (request_id, symbol, window_start...); (4) every producer INSERT fails → the worker batch-salvage dead-letters every row, repeating on every restart.
- **Tests**: test_perf_deadletter_skeleton_repro; tests/unit/test_perf_deadletter_skeleton_repro.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/database/app_columns.py; nexus_scalp/database/engine.py; nexus_scalp/database/manifest.py; src/nexus_scalp/database/app_columns.py; src/nexus_scalp/database/engine.py; src/nexus_scalp/database/manifest.py

## BUG-255 - audit_dead_letter grew without bound (553k rows / ~1GB in 15h): no retention cap on the diagnostic dead-letter table

- **Severity**: HIGH (unbounded diagnostic growth on a 3.8GB VM; dead-letter is diagnostic evidence, NOT financial truth, but its volume must be bounded) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/dead_letter_store.py` (DeadLetterStore.record/_prune_if_due/_prune_locked), `src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/audit_repository.py` (facade dead_letter_pruned_rows), `src/nexus_scalp/web/debug_snapshot.py` (audit_dead_letter_pruned_rows)
- **Root Cause**: BUG-254 produced a permanent failure loop (~10 rows/s); the dead-letter table had NO cap, NO prune, NO age policy — audit.db grew ~1GB/15h.
- **Fix**: bounded retention on record() — keep the NEWEST max_rows (default 20,000 ≈ 36MB at the measured ~1.8KB/row), prune throttled to ≤1 pass/30s (None sentinel: first pass always due — monotonic-boot trap), batched rowid-anchored short transactions (WAL-safe, never one giant DELETE), live-COUNT as source of truth, ONE WARNING per overflow event (re-armed under cap), dead_letter_pruned_rows surfaced on the facade + debug_snapshot. Retention deletes from audit_dead_letter ONLY — pinned: ledger row, account snapshot and the persisted HALT row survive a full overflow cycle.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_dead_letter_retention_cap.py (5) + test_perf_safety_contract_pins.py (HALT survives; LIVE/PAPER provenance columns intact).
- **Invariants**: INV-023 (cleanup bounded/off-tick-path, never financial truth), INV-025 (halt semantics untouched)
- **Tests**: test_dead_letter_retention_cap; test_perf_safety_contract_pins; tests/unit/test_dead_letter_retention_cap.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/adapters/database/audit_repository.py; nexus_scalp/adapters/database/dead_letter_store.py; nexus_scalp/web/debug_snapshot.py; src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/audit_repository.py; src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/dead_letter_store.py; src/nexus_scalp/web/debug_snapshot.py
- **Invariants**: INV-023, INV-025

## BUG-256 - audit_executions UNIQUE-index bootstrap crashed startup on every pre-release database: bare CREATE UNIQUE INDEX against legacy duplicate rows

- **Severity**: CRITICAL (AuditRepository.__init__ raised for EVERY construction on any DB created before the agent-17 idempotency release — engine boot, web console, CLI doctor/smoke/repair all dead; multiple independent environments hit it after the same release → systemic migration defect, not a dirty-DB one-off) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/audit_repository.py::_create_sqlite_tables` (bare `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_executions_order_status ON audit_executions (order_id, status)`, added by c2662e31), NEW `src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/executions_idempotency.py`
- **Symptom**: `sqlite3.IntegrityError: UNIQUE constraint failed: audit_executions.order_id, audit_executions.status` at `_create_sqlite_tables` during construction. Live evidence on this host's production artifacts/audit.db (957 rows): 3 duplicate groups (test_req_1/2/3 REJECTED x319 — the BUG-223 test_req contamination burst 08-31..09-02 written before the identity existed); identical failure reproduced from that file.
- **Fix**: `ensure_executions_idempotency_index(conn)` (stdlib-only helper) — bounded detect (≤500 duplicate identities/attempt, mirrors `_ORDERS_DEDUP_REPAIR_BATCH`) → NON-DESTRUCTIVE reconcile (superseded rows archived VERBATIM with `original_id`/`reconciled_at`/`reconcile_reason` to `audit_executions_reconciled`; survivor = LOWEST id = earliest observation, same rule as the audit_orders repair) → `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`. Transactional per pass (BEGIN IMMEDIATE or the driver's implicit immediate txn; busy_timeout 10s for cross-process wait); one retry-after-repair converts a cross-process race into convergence; interrupted-partial-repair DBs recover (archive is authoritative; already-archived-but-live rows leave the table without re-archiving). A still-duplicated remainder fails LOUDLY — the engine never boots silently without the dedup identity. No DB deletion, no rename, no operator hand-repair required.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug254_executions_idempotency_migration.py (8): legacy-dirty migrate + construction succeeds, 3x idempotent re-run identical state, fresh-DB double-init noop, concurrent init (4 workers: no corruption, no double-archive, row conservation), interrupted-migration recovery, no-row-lost integrity across live+archive, writer redelivery semantics (same status collapses / distinct status inserts), bounded-repair + loud remainder. E2E: production audit.db COPY that crashes under the old DDL constructs in 2.5s with live(3)+archived(954)=957, dup_groups=0, integrity ok, audit_ledger/audit_signals untouched.
- **Note**: sweep of every other CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bootstrap on this DB (audit_signals dedup, audit_orders execution_id, paper-exec identity, model/strategy registry UNIQUEs) found 0 duplicate groups — audit_executions was the only unguarded site; `idx_orders_execution_idempotency` already had its repair.
- **Invariants**: INV-005/006 (duplicate-event idempotency — now actually enforced), INV-007 (historical rows immutable — superseded rows …
- **Tests**: test_bug254_executions_idempotency_migration; test_req; test_req_1; tests/unit/test_bug254_executions_idempotency_migration.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/adapters/database/audit_repository.py; nexus_scalp/adapters/database/executions_idempotency.py; src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/audit_repository.py; src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/executions_idempotency.py
- **Invariants**: INV-001, INV-005, INV-007
- **Commits**: c2662e31
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8430

## BUG-259 - Hold-score Time-in-Loss penalty derived its holding-duration denominator from the HOST WALL CLOCK (datetime.now) instead of the tick-threaded …

- **Severity**: MEDIUM (money-path exit scoring: a host wall clock hours behind the broker/tick domain near-zeroed holding_duration and fired the spurious -30 TIME_IN_LOSS_DECAY_PENALTY on fresh positions; the opposite skew inflated the duration and suppressed the penalty forever — the same mixed-clock-domain class that produced production "Age: -10781.6s" and drove the G3 fix) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/execution/lifecycle/scoring.py` (_calculate_hold_value_score Penalty 2, was lines 412-421), `src/nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py` (_evaluate_hold_score signature + manage-loop call site, delegate passthrough unchanged)
- **Root Cause**: the TASK-AUDREV-G3-HOLD-CLOCK fix removed the wall-clock fallback from _evaluate_minimum_loss_optimization/_arbitrate_decision and threaded now=tick.timestamp through the management loop, but the S9-extracted _calculate_hold_value_score kept its own private clock: `datetime.now()` (naive entry) / `datetime.now(UTC)` (aware entry) against om._entry_timestamps, and _evaluate_hold_score (order_manager.py:3313-3335) never forwarded the loop's `now`. Entry anchors themselves are broker-domain (pos.time_setup at order_manager.py:2738), so the two domains disagree whenever host-vs-broker skew, DST, or an NTP step occurs.
- **Fix**: `now: datetime | None = None` threaded from the manage loop (now = current_tick.timestamp, order_manager.py:2687) through _evaluate_hold_score into _calculate_hold_value_score; duration = (now - entry_time) ONLY when both share the same naive/aware domain (no silent mixed-domain subtraction); no usable now -> conservative 0.0 (the >0.70 ratio gate can never fire on corrupted arithmetic) + rate-limited (300s) loud WARNING event=HOLD_AGE_FALLBACK, mirroring G3. Delegate order_manager.py:3977 passes kwargs through unchanged.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug259_hold_score_hold_clock.py (7 tests: tick-domain ratio drives the penalty, ratio follows tick now not wall clock, no-now conservative zero + single rate-limited warning, manage-loop threading proof via spy, delegate passthrough, static no-datetime.now guard); tests/unit/test_rule_matrix.py::test_dynamic_hold_score_calculation updated to pass the tick stamp explicitly (RED pre-fix at af2bf200: expected 70 got 100); test_rule_matrix.py::test_api_endpoints and test_exit_separation_w3.py::_tick pre-existing wall-clock/maintenance-window flakes fixed in passing (NSE_WEB_AUTH_DISABLE fixture opt-out; tick pinned to a fixed mid-session stamp). Evidence: new suite 7/7 GREEN post-fix, 7/7 RED pre-fix (git stash probe on the worktree); 95 passed across the 12-suite hold-score/engine lane; ruff check+format clean (1030 files); mypy clean on both touched src files.
- **Invariants**: INV-008 (no lookahead untouched), _process_tick_pipeline not blocked (throttle + giveback override structure unchanged), no gateway/client contract touched, domain models untouched.
- **Tests**: test_api_endpoints; test_bug259_hold_score_hold_clock; test_dynamic_hold_score_calculation; test_exit_separation_w3; test_rule_matrix; tests/unit/test_bug259_hold_score_hold_clock.py; tests/unit/test_rule_matrix.py
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/execution/lifecycle/scoring.py; nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py; src/nexus_scalp/execution/lifecycle/scoring.py; src/nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py
- **Invariants**: INV-008
- **Commits**: af2bf200

## BUG-263 - Rollback restored .previous-* snapshots WITHOUT integrity re-verification: tampered/corrupted snapshot bytes were silently activated over the live …

- **Severity**: CRITICAL. Component: release/update_engine/rollback_state.py (+ release/packaging.py, update_engine/orchestrator.py).
- **Symptom**: `nexus update rollback` and the automatic rollback paths copied every child of a `.previous-*` snapshot over the live app tree with zero verification (plain shutil.move loop). Modified/planted snapshot bytes (malware, mistaken rsync, corruption, hand-edit) were activated verbatim — rollback was an integrity bypass around the whole update trust chain (Ed25519 signed update-manifest -> payload SHA-256 -> in-payload release-manifest.json -> BUG-166 pre-stage embed), because the chain verified the payload INBOUND and nothing re-verified the snapshot at RESTORE time. Baseline: tests/unit/test_agent9_rollback_integrity.py (md5 fe4c63dd7b405dce0f90348e49c06b57) 4/5 RED on clean main.
- **Root Cause**: restore_application trusted the snapshot's existence as proof of integrity; the release-manifest.json CI embeds in the installed tree (release.yml embed step / Inno {app} copy of manifests/) was never consulted when restoring.
- **Fix**: restore_application fully re-verifies BEFORE copying anything (verify-then-copy; no partial restore): locate the embedded manifest (root or manifests/ spelling, packaging.locate_embedded_manifest), parse via the canonical reader packaging.manifest_records (BOTH production shapes: release-root `artifacts` records, portable-root `files` mapping), reject traversal/absolute/malformed-digest entries structurally before hashing (manifest_entry_escape_reason), recompute SHA-256 of every listed file through packaging.verify_manifest (reuses the BUG-166 chain's parsing + sha256_file hashing — no second source of truth), cross-check an embedded SHA256SUMS.txt via verify_checksums_file, and manifest version/git_commit vs the tree's build-info.json (manifest_identity_conflict = the fields verify-release._identity_check compares). Anti-shadowing: when a record resolves under several BUG-160 spellings (portable/x and x), ALL in-tree spellings must match the claim, so a planted twin cannot vouch for a tampered restored file. Any failure -> {"restored": False, "error_code": "SNAPSHOT_INTEGRITY_FAILED"} (absent manifest: "SNAPSHOT_NO_MANIFEST") with the live tree byte-unchanged; the orchestrator maps a refusal to FAILED_SAFE + ROLLBACK_REFUSED history (never a fake ROLLED_BACK), and the post-update-verification auto-rollback reports rollback_completed=False + FAILED. Layout tolerance without hash tolerance: release-root records that can never live in an app tree (cli/*, payload *.zip, *-setup.exe) and the preserved user-data dirs (artifacts/data/logs — never restored) are tolerated-as-absent but reported; PRESENCE still forces hash equality.
- **Regression**: spec battery tests/unit/test_agent9_rollback_integrity.py (5, byte-exact pinned fixture, kept md5-stable via …
- **Tests**: test_agent9_rollback_integrity; test_bug263_snapshot_integrity_gate; tests/unit/test_agent9_rollback_integrity.py; tests/unit/test_bug263_snapshot_integrity_gate.py
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8462

A +180min pin step can land EXACTLY on an inclusive edge: from now in [16:30:00, 16:30:59] UTC the first probe hits 19:30:xx (in-window), the single retry hits 22:30:xx (in-window), and the guard blocks the first dispatch, so `assert dispatch_order(...) is True` fails. All five test files now pin through the helper (or a fixed mid-session stamp where freshness is irrelevant).
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug264_maintenance_pin_collision.py (6, critical_suite-gated): the exact CI-1085 instant re-demonstrates the legacy collision (RED-before pinned), second-resolution day sweep proves the red band is EXACTLY 16:30:00..16:30:59 and the helper never returns an in-window pin (86400 samples), minute-resolution day sweep, +-90s edge sweeps at all three anchor instants, untouched-when-outside, fail-loud on impossible geometry. ## BUG-270 - Alt-UI standalone host echoed client/upstream header bytes raw: 9 CodeQL py/http-response-splitting alerts on main

- **Severity**: MEDIUM (CodeQL security-severity 6.1, precision high; alerts #1086-#1094 on refs/heads/main, tool 2.27.0). The stdlib email parser that ``http.server`` uses PRESERVES obs-fold continuations inside a header VALUE (``X-Request-ID: abc\n\tSet-Cookie: ...`` parses to ``"abc\n\tSet-Cookie: ..."`` — probe on CPython 3.11), and ``BaseHTTPRequestHandler.send_header`` buffers ``"%s: %s\r\n" % (kw, val)`` and latin-1-encodes …
- **Surface**: scripts/serve_alt_ui.py — _dispatch (request-id), _send_static_file, _relay stream+buffered header loops, HEAD content-length echo, _send_error_envelope. Alerts: py/http-response-splitting #1086-#1094 (analysis .github/workflows/security.yml:codeql).
- **Evidence**: CodeQL DB from scripts/serve_alt_ui.py at origin/main b0e34cef -> 9 results on exactly lines 386/531x2/536/549x2/554/559/652 == alerts #1086-#1094; re-run on the fixed file -> 0 results. ruff check + ruff format --check clean repo-wide (1967 files); mypy clean on the file; full standalone-alt-UI suite 48 passed.
- **Fix**: layered, stdlib-only, no contract loss. (1) source: correlation id charset-allowlisted at ``_dispatch`` via ``request_id_is_safe`` (``[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]``, max 64; anything else => fresh ``req_`` id, never echoed); (2) relay names: upstream header names must pass ``field_name_is_safe`` (RFC 9110 tchar set, max 128) or are dropped (production uvicorn/h11 never emits non-token names, so relay parity is unaffected); (3) every relayed/echoed name+value passes ``header_safe()`` (CR/LF strip) — the sanitizer shape CodeQL models as a barrier; (4) choke point: handler overrides ``send_header`` to strip CR/LF from BOTH args, so no future echo regresses the invariant; (5) HEAD relay echoes ``Content-Length`` only when ``.isdigit()``. Validators are deliberately PLAIN set/length checks, NOT regexes: a regex fed attacker-controlled bytes is itself a scanning target — PR #184's first push drew a NEW ``py/polynomial-redos`` (HIGH) alert on ``_FIELD_NAME_RE.fullmatch(name)`` over upstream bytes (CodeQL flags per-char class scanning of unbounded input); commit 2 replaced both regexes with linear checks. Verified with CodeQL CLI 2.27.0 + the exact codeql/python-queries ``HeaderInjection.ql`` AND ``PolynomialReDoS.ql`` run LOCALLY: pre-fix file reproduced all 9 header-splitting alerts at the alert lines; final file = 0 alerts on both queries.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_alt_ui_standalone_server.py TestHeaderInjectionDefense (8): header_safe unit, id-charset + field-name validator units (+ no-regex-on-attacker-bytes pin), send_header choke-point (raw _headers_buffer inspection), folded request-id never echoed (raw-socket obs-fold request; upstream receives only the clean minted id), spacey id mints replacement, upstream fold + illegal-name never split the relayed head (new stub /api/smuggle literal route, line-level wire assertions), HEAD relay echoes only a numeric content-length. RED-before (fix reverted via git show HEAD file): 6/7 FAIL; post-fix 49/49 green — the 41 existing echo/relay/traversal/SSE/fail-closed/redaction pins …
- **Invariants**: correlation contract preserved (legitimate ids still honored/echoed/forwarded — pinned by existing tests); hop-by-hop …
- **Tests**: test_alt_ui_standalone_server; tests/unit/test_alt_ui_standalone_server.py
- **Commits**: b0e34cef
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8521

## BUG-272 - Champion-drift sentinel: the P0-2 trust anchor fires at BOOT only, so an out-of-process rewrite of the serving artifact while the engine runs is …

- **Severity**: P2 (detection gap on a fail-closed system — the enforcement is correct, the blind window is not) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/model_lifecycle/champion_sentinel.py` (new), `src/nexus_scalp/application/live/maintenance.py` (stage), `src/nexus_scalp/application/live/model_bundle_store.py::_verify_champion_registry_binding` (boot-only anchor, byte-unchanged), `src/nexus_scalp/application/live_engine.py:2116` (`_maintenance.run_cycle` on the runtime path).
- **Symptom**: BUG-257's writer is still unidentified, and BUG-271 only made GOVERNED writes attributable. If any foreign process (editor, sync tool, an old launcher holding a reference to the quarantined bb1f0afe copy) re-lands drifted bytes on `artifacts/models/scalp//model.pt` while the engine runs, the running engine keeps serving unverified weights; the refusal fires only at the NEXT cold boot — after unbounded trading on poisoned bytes. The 09-11 re-publication at 04:13 proves the write happens; nothing observes it until restart.
- **Evidence**: this run — sentinel battery 11 passed RC=0 (slim venv, torch present); red-before shape: the stage did not exist at 3ad2b85f (grep probe_champion_drift src -> only the new module). Neighbor lanes: test_learnfix1_provenance_binding 6 passed (LEARNFIX1-TRIV-1 fixed same run), bug271 11 passed, critical_suite meta green (every manifest path exists at HEAD). ruff check + format repo-wide clean; mypy clean on maintenance.py + champion_sentinel.py.
- **Fix**: alert-only between-boot sentinel. `champion_sentinel.evaluate_champion_drift` (PURE: sha256-prefix-16 of serving bytes vs the newest CHAMPION row fingerprint — the exact anchor comparison, so a DRIFT verdict and a boot refusal can never disagree; MATCH/DRIFT/INERT mirror the anchor postures, cold states are INERT not alarms) + `probe_champion_drift` (I/O via the URI-aware `audit._connect_sqlite` single connect site, registry read + fingerprint, never raises). Wired into `MaintenanceCycle.run_cycle` (lazy, off the tick path via asyncio.to_thread, fully failure-isolated): None "never ran" throttle sentinel (first pass always due — the `_last=0.0` vs monotonic trap), 15-min interval, TWO consecutive DRIFT sightings before one CRITICAL log + Telegram (absorbs the governed-writer window where the file write precedes the FIFO-queued supersession), re-alarm every 4th sighting, CHAMPION_DRIFT_CLEARED on resolution, INERT resets silently. NEVER mutates/halts — INV-015 enforcement stays the boot anchor; the module stays torch/polars-free at import (maintenance-path gating lesson).
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug257_champion_drift_sentinel.py (11, gated in critical_suite.txt): pure verdict table, real-DB probe match/drift/INERT postures, DRIFT<=>anchor-REFUSE equivalence through the …
- **Ledger**: closes the BUG-257 "REMAINING: alerting between boots for OUT-OF-PROCESS writes" row …

## BUG-273 - HOLD_AGE_FALLBACK rate limiter used the `_last=0.0` vs time.monotonic() throttle pattern: the FIRST warning is silently suppressed on any host with …

- **Severity**: P3 (observability of the fail-safe itself: the conservative-zero behavior stays correct, but its loud warning vanishes exactly when the engine was just restarted — the moment the fallback is most likely to fire) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/execution/lifecycle/scoring.py` (:435 hold-value score, :526 minimum-loss grace), `src/nexus_scalp/execution/order_manager.py:2417` (management-loop age fallback).
- **Root Cause**: the G3/BUG-259/BUG-268 wave rate-limited HOLD_AGE_FALLBACK with `if (time.monotonic() - getattr(self, "_hold_age_fallback_warned_at", 0.0)) >= 300.0` — `time.monotonic()` starts at 0 at BOOT, so on a host whose uptime is below the interval the comparison is False and the FIRST warning never emits. The swarm has already been bitten by this exact shape once (memory: "Throttle-sentinel trap ... Use a None 'never ran' sentinel") — the dead-letter retention prune fixed it there; the hold-age trio kept it.

- **Regression**: NEW `test_fresh_host_monotonic_uptime_first_fallback_still_warns` in tests/unit/test_bug268_min_loss_grace_clock.py — fake module clock at t=120s (boot 2 min ago): first fallback warns (RED before fix on a low-uptime host), 4 more calls inside the window add nothing, re-arm after 300s emits again. - **Symptom (Real Ci Evidence)**: `tests/unit/test_bug268_min_loss_grace_clock.py::test_no_now_fallback_warns_rate_limited` FAILED on the ubuntu quality job (run 34788680324, head b5743962) with "expected exactly 1 rate-limited warning, got 0" — intermittent by host uptime (passes once monotonic() crosses 300 after runner boot). The test is NOT machine-state-dependent by design; the production code is.
