## BUG-275 - DB-hygiene cadence clock-domain mix: RuntimeCleanupScheduler stamped _last_light/_last_deep with time.monotonic() while its ONLY production caller …

- **Severity**: P2 (resource/IO waste on a protective background path + misleading status; no trading-behavior impact) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/hygiene/hygiene_runtime.py` (cadence block :149-168, run_cycle stamps :232-236, mark_telegram_sent :396, status :404), caller `src/nexus_scalp/application/live/maintenance.py:254-259` (`now_t = time.time()` from :74).
- **Symptom**: (1) deep index-health scans (`_index_health_report`: read-only sqlite opens + PRAGMA index scans over all MANAGED_DATABASES) fired EVERY light cycle (30 min) instead of every 6h — 12x intended IO on the engine host; (2) `status()`/`next_light_in()` (default now=monotonic against wall stamps and vice versa) reported nonsense `next_light_in_sec` on the `nexus db hygiene` CLI panel + /api/db/hygiene runtime block. Telegram cooldown: production-safe only because maintenance passes now_t explicitly to mark_telegram_sent; the no-arg default kept the mixed-domain hazard.
- **Root Cause**: the G3/BUG-259/BUG-268 clock-domain discipline was applied per-module: each side stayed internally consistent, but the scheduler's stamps (monotonic, boot-uptime scale ~1e5-1e6) crossed a domain boundary at the CALLER comparison (`now_wall - _last_deep >= deep_interval` with now_wall ~1.79e9 -> always True). Same class the swarm pinned for /health caching and worker cadence (wall workers use 0.0 sentinel FINE because the domain matches; mixing the domains is the bug).
- **Evidence**: this run — bug275+bug274+operational-log-hygiene+storage-wiring 40 passed RC=0; database_hygiene_task11 + agent16_w2 + calendar_maintenance + bug268 lane 36 passed RC=0; ruff check/format repo-wide clean (1989 files); mypy Success hygiene_runtime.py.
- **Fix**: single documented contract — ALL cadence stamps + comparisons in the scheduler use the WALL clock (`time.time()`), the domain the engine's maintenance tick supplies; duration measurement inside run_cycle correctly STAYS monotonic. 4 sites: run_cycle stamps, next_light_in default, mark_telegram_sent default, status now.
- **Regression**: NEW tests/unit/test_bug275_hygiene_cadence_clock.py (5, gated): wall-domain stamp pins, deep-gate-respects-interval (RED-BEFORE proven at fd95a7b0 baseline worktree: 4/5 RED pre-fix, incl. the permanent-due assertion), telegram wall default, next_light_in/status sanity, md7-style wiring pin (maintenance supplies wall now_t + scheduler must never stamp monotonic cadence).
- **Tests**: test_bug275_hygiene_cadence_clock; tests/unit/test_bug275_hygiene_cadence_clock.py

## BUG-276 - Baseline-skeleton CONSTRAINT shadow (PERF-DEADLETTER remainder): gate-first fresh DBs have no UNIQUE indexes for 15 of the 17 ON CONFLICT producers …

- **Severity**: P1 (forensic-data loss on money-path tables: experience ledger, guard telemetry, strategy registry, research runs — learning-loop evidence destroyed silently on every fresh install) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/database/engine.py` (`_create_baseline_tables`), `src/nexus_scalp/adapters/database/audit_repository.py` (bootstrap + new `_ensure_unique_constraint_heal`), `src/nexus_scalp/database/app_columns.py` (new `APP_UNIQUE_TARGETS` contract).
- **Root Cause**: the 2026-09-10 PERF-DEADLETTER wave healed baseline-skeleton tables to the app's COLUMN contract (APP_REQUIRED_COLUMNS) but left the CONSTRAINT half of the shadow intact: a skeleton carries only `id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY`, the app's UNIQUE constraints are unreachable (CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS no-op; ADD COLUMN cannot add an index), and SQLite refuses `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT(<cols>)` outright — "ON CONFLICT clause does not match any PRIMARY KEY or UNIQUE constraint" — when no UNIQUE/PK index covers exactly `<cols>`. The batch-recovery salvage loop then dead-letters EVERY row of every affected producer, forever, at producer cadence. audit_signals (idx_audit_signals_dedup), audit_orders (idx_orders_execution_idempotency) and audit_executions (ensure_executions_idempotency_index) self-heal their indexes inside the app bootstrap — the remaining 15 targets had no repair path.
- **Evidence**: live in the 2026-09-14 nightly Client E2E container (run 34820773492, job 103901618864): `Audit batch insert failed ... dead_lettered=19 error=ON CONFLICT clause does not match any PRIMARY KEY or UNIQUE constraint` at ~1/s from the first tick (audit_guard_telemetry via TICK_DUPLICATE_SUPPRESSED). Locally reproduced at HEAD 79401911 with the exact production sequence (gate then AuditRepository bootstrap on a fresh DB): 15 producer upserts FAIL (behavior_analysis/audit_experiences/audit_experience_outcomes/experience_model_registry/strategy_registry/research_runs/research_worker_state/strategy_intelligence_registry/position_lifecycle_events/trade_autopsies/behavior_detections/anomaly_events/strategy_evolution_candidates/intelligence_worker_state/audit_guard_telemetry); audit_ledger + the three self-healing tables pass. The repo-wide ON-CONFLICT scanner (now pinned as a class guard) matches 21 audit-DB targets, same result.
- **Fix**: (1) `APP_UNIQUE_TARGETS` (app_columns.py, stdlib-only, machine-readable copy of the producers' ON CONFLICT targets); (2) engine baseline builds the unique indexes at gate time (tables are empty inside the same transaction — fail-loud, `BASELINE_UNIQUE_HEALED` event); (3) `AuditRepository._ensure_unique_constraint_heal` runs at the END of `_create_sqlite_tables` on every boot, repairing EXISTING poisoned databases (the field shape: DB already built by the pre-fix engine): PRAGMA-gated zero-churn on healthy DBs (app-first boot …
- **Tests**: test_bug276_unique_constraint_shadow; test_perf_deadletter_skeleton_repro; tests/unit/test_bug276_unique_constraint_shadow.py

- **Commits**: 79401911
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8583

## BUG-277 - `nexus doctor` WARNING-actions filter matched the RENDERED label "WARN" against the producer vocabulary "WARNING" — the "USER ACTION / NEXT" …

- **Severity**: P2 (operator observability: the closing panel that answers "what do I do next?" silently under-reports and can send an operator to `nexus start` on a migration-behind audit.db) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/cli/doctor.py:235` (was `warns = [e for e in entries if e.verdict == "WARN"]`), consumed by the closing panel at :256-273 (`USER ACTION: n` count + `NEXT: <suggestion>` line). Producers: `HealthEntry.verdict` is PASS|WARNING|FAIL (`src/nexus_scalp/release/health.py:99`, 37 `"WARNING"` sites); the rendered label "WARN" exists ONLY in `src/nexus_scalp/cli/styling.py:_verdict_style` ("WARNING" -> "[yellow]WARN[/yellow]").
- **Root Cause**: vocabulary mismatch between the display layer and the data layer — the filter compares the raw field against a string only the renderer ever produces. Same class as the swarm's throttle-sentinel and wrapper-state findings: a comparison that is syntactically fine and semantically dead. The 2026-09-02 "actionable closing summary" UX pass was built on top of this permanently-empty list, so it only ever showed FAIL-derived counts; the `elif user_n:` branch (first WARNING suggestion as NEXT) was unreachable code.
- **Fix**: filter matches the producer vocabulary — `e.verdict in ("WARNING", "WARN")` (legacy label kept for any external entry source). Adjacent drift fix in the same commit: the banner literal "system doctor · 24 checks" -> `f"... {len(entries)} checks"` (run_all() is the SSOT; 26 check methods exist, 24 wired — the literal is exactly the staleness class this wave keeps proving).
- **Regression**: NEW tests/unit/test_bug277_doctor_warning_vocabulary.py (8, gated in critical_suite): 4 behavior pins via CliRunner with `_health_entries` stubbed (actionable WARNING reaches USER ACTION/NEXT — RED-BEFORE 4/4 proven at baseline be1842d2 in a worktree; multi-warning count; clean-state keeps `nexus start`; no-suggestion WARNINGs do not inflate), producer-vocabulary contract (run_all() never emits "WARN"), styling source-pin ("WARNING" -> "WARN" mapping lives in the renderer), CLASS GUARD regex over all of src/: no `verdict ==/!= "WARN"` comparison may return (the two legitimate `status == "WARN"` users — smoke runner, release-verify — own a different PASS|FAIL|WARN|SKIP contract, excluded by keying on the field name), md7-style source-pin of the fixed filter text.
- **Symptom**: host-independent): any DEGRADED install whose guidance lives in WARNING entries — `DATABASE` "schema 8 behind expected 9 → Run `nexus db migrate`", `NEWS` disabled, `GPU` NVIDIA-without-CUDA, `LOGGING`/`SHADOW`/`MEMORY` suggestions — prints `USER ACTION: 0` and `NEXT: nexus start (paper mode by default)`. On a migration-behind ledger that is precisely the BUG-254/276 dead-letter pre-condition, told to the operator by the tool designed to catch it. The `--fix` path is unaffected (FAIL-derived) …
- **Evidence (This Run)**: battery 8 passed RC=0 (post-fix), baseline worktree 4/8 RED pre-fix incl. both behavior shapes + both source pins …

## BUG-285 - Durable financial-overflow was WRITE-ONLY (perf-wave R2): an overflowed audit row never returned to the ledger and the directory grew unbounded

- **Severity**: P1 (accounting evidence loss by design gap: the "durable overflow file" third line of defense had NO reader anywhere in src/ — a financial row that overflowed stayed on disk forever, invisible to every ledger consumer (reports, risk chain, forensics), while the directory grew one unpruned file per overflow event) | STATUS: FIXED

On top: no bound on pending files (disk growth under sustained saturation), no poison handling, and `get_runtime_workspace() / _FINANCIAL_OVERFLOW_DIR` was hand-built at two places (a future reader could resolve a different path and never find the files).

- **Fix**: bounded, idempotent, worker-thread-only): (1) `_overflow_dir()` single resolution site — writer, drainer, and the public `overflow_pending_count()` all share it; (2) `_drain_financial_overflow_due(conn)` runs from the audit worker's IDLE pass only (queue produced no batch — a busy writer never pays the scan; never the tick path, INV-001): cadence-gated <=1 pass/60s (None sentinel — first pass always due, BUG-273 class), batch <=500 files oldest-first, replay of the stored INSERT/REPLACE + args on the worker's OWN connection (single writer; producer SQL is all ON CONFLICT idempotent, so a duplicate replay is a no-op, never a double count); (3) retire = rename-then-delete under `overflow_recovered/` (storage-hygiene discipline — a crash between replay and unlink cannot lose the row twice); (4) poison (non-INSERT …
- **Evidence (This Run)**: RED-BEFORE proven at ddf5c946 baseline worktree (battery fails on the absent API), GREEN at HEAD: bug278 16+2 RC=0 …

## BUG-278 - Repo-root pytest CLOBBERED the production champion bundle + contaminated the live model registry with CHAMPION rows

- **Symptom**: artifacts/models/scalp/XAUUSD/70d_liquidity/model.pt flipped governed c9982ddde1755591 -> fresh-init bb1f0afe30f746da during `pytest tests/unit -n 4` from the repo root (observed twice: lane-05 audit 03:14Z, and the 09-11 class); registry rows 4073 (CHAMPION bound to a pytest tmp_path model.pt!) and 4080 (CHAMPION on clobbered bytes) — the P0-2 trust anchor reads the LATEST CHAMPION row, so contamination poisons boot trust in both directions.
- **Fix**: load_database_config honors NEXUS_AUDIT_DB for the implicit sqlite default (audit domain only; NSE_DATABASE__*/PG precedence unchanged); ModelBundleStore._load_or_create_bundle refuses force_fresh on an EXISTING artifact inside the governed models tree (release.paths.get_models_dir) — fail-closed RuntimeError, called UNBOUND per the mixin contract (BUG-274 class avoided); violating tests now mint into tmp paths; src call-site ban pin (production never passes force_fresh=True). Quarantine: scripts/maintenance/quarantine_test_champion_rows.py — row-scoped, evidence-preserving, idempotent, dry-run default; applied: rows 4073 (R1 tmp-path champion) + 4080 (R2 clobbered-bytes champion) -> QUARANTINED with BUG-278 reasons; governed row 4156 (c9982ddd) survives as sole latest CHAMPION; JSON evidence in docs/audit/wave_20260914/bug278_quarantine_evidence.json.
- **Operator Note**: NEVER run repo-root unit suites while a production engine uses artifacts/ (the harness writes settings/paper state even post-fix for non-audit stores; prefer isolated workspace or CI layout). Lane-05 disclosure (Z-B2) independently reproduced the clobber; its one-time manual restore is superseded by this fix + registry quarantine.
- **Root Cause (Two Holes)**: (1) tests/conftest.py BUG-223 isolation sets NEXUS_AUDIT_DB, but LiveEngine's IMPLICIT audit construction resolves via database/config.load_database_config -> default_sqlite_path -> PRODUCTION artifacts/audit.db — the env seam was honored only in AuditRepository's legacy implicit default; test engines then wrote rows into the live registry. (2) LiveEngine(force_fresh_model=True) minted UNTRAINED weights and WROTE them over config.model.model_artifact_path (the production bundle) — 3 test files used force_fresh WITHOUT isolating the path (c3_spread_session_wiring, runtime_config_api, download_ready e2e); champion_sync/register_model then stamped whatever bytes were on disk.
- **Tests**: test_bug278_test_harness_artifact_isolation; tests/conftest.py; tests/unit/test_bug278_test_harness_artifact_isolation.py

## BUG-279 - Feed stall watchdog warned forever and NEVER changed state: 09-11T20:00Z freeze ran 2.5 days with runtime_risk_state=RUNNING + a HIGH incident …

- **Root Cause**: watchdog remediation (resubscribe/reconnect/_resync) ran per-15s but the STALL STATE was owned by nobody: the incident emission was per-pass spam, the stall clock was the watchdog's own resettable _last_tick_processed_time (reset by its own timer path), and no consumer ever blocked entries or surfaced DEGRADED for a stalled feed.
- **Fix**: engine-owned stall episode (LiveEngine.note_tick_stream_stall/note_tick_stream_recovered): one EPISODE_OPEN incident (MT5_DISCONNECTED kept distinct on the disconnected branch), one CRITICAL escalation past 900s freshness.stall_grace_sec: runtime_risk_state() -> DEGRADED (UI truthful via runtime_risk_state_effective), _trading_blocked_by_safety_state -> True (BUG-256 dispatch gates block NEW ENTRIES; protective exits untouched), Telegram CRITICAL, critical log. Stall clock = wall time of last NEW tick, stamped only AFTER _process_tick_pipeline (duplicate early-return never resets it). FOREX-weekend suppression (Fri 22:00..Sun 22:00 UTC, market_calendar convention): closed market is quiet, not stalled. Recovery event closes the episode; new episodes re-escalate.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug279_feed_stall_fail_loud.py (8: below-grace single START, past-grace exactly-once CRITICAL + DEGRADED + entry-block via provider, weekend never escalates, recovery resets + re-arms, disconnected escalates, loop source-pins stamp ordering) — critical_suite-pinned. NEIGHBORS: bug256 dispatch halt (8) + runtime_safety_mission (31) green; mypy/ruff clean.
- **Residual**: watchdog still only RESUBSCRIBES on stall; if resync can never recover (terminal up, pipe dead — the 09-11 shape), operator intervention remains required (escalation now LOUD: DEGRADED + Telegram + CRITICAL incident, was: silent). A future auto-restart policy is an operator decision, deliberately NOT added (fail-loud != auto-kill).
- **Symptom**: 2): last audit_signals row 2026-09-11T19:59:59Z; boots 09-12..09-14 log '[WATCHDOG] Tick stream stalled while MT5 reports connected' 1,828+ times (09-13) and every ~20s (09-14); /health engine section could still report READY-adjacent; runtime_risk_state 'RUNNING'; the BUG-169 duplicate-tick early-return skips features/policy/risk/dispatch entirely on the frozen quote, so ZERO decisions are produced while the engine 'reports healthy'. Current passivity is NOT rejections — it is dead plumbing invisible in every rejection table.
- **Tests**: test_bug279_feed_stall_fail_loud; tests/unit/test_bug279_feed_stall_fail_loud.py
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8624

- **Surface**: src/nexus_scalp/signals/policy.py _evaluate_frequency_throttle (early return at evaluate_probabilities:359) vs the post-policy COOLDOWN_ACTIVE gate (:1276) reading the SAME self._last_signal_time with the CONFIGURED self.cooldown_seconds (engine passes 4.0, live_engine.py:1391).

Protective AI-reversal re-entries share the same clock (throttle runs BEFORE candidate construction).
- **Fix**: the throttle now reads policy.cooldown_seconds — ONE authoritative cooldown policy; strictness follows configuration (a configured 60 still blocks at 60 — no threshold loosened; the magic literal dies). 4s-class configured values restore the designed micro-scap evaluation cadence.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug280_frequency_throttle_unification.py (5: strict-config-still-blocks, configured-fast honored RED-before pin for the 60s hardcode incl. inside-window throttle on a distinct quote, source-pin no 60.0 literal, both-sites-one-policy, engine-passes-config) — critical_suite-pinned. BUG-227 throttle pins rewritten against cooldown_seconds (the window contract moved from literal to configuration; guard NOT weakened: block/release behavior unchanged at the configured boundary). test_obs_trace_chain2 dedupe-vs-throttle window adjusted to the configured 3s default. NEIGHBORS: policy_throttle/obs_trace/bug054-growth/spread_gates_c3/c3_wiring 57 passed; mypy src clean; ruff clean.

## BUG-282 - RSS pubDate (RFC-822) silently fabricated to ingest now(): 88.1% of news rows carry a fake event time AND the 60-s publish bucket in the article-hash …

- **Regression**: tests/unit/news/ingest/test_bug282_rfc822_and_identity.py (10, critical_suite-pinned): RFC-822 GMT/-0400/+0200/EST parse to the true instant (RED-before -> now()), ISO paths unchanged, unparseable -> None (RED-before: fabricated wall clock), timeless-hash poll-stability + timed 60-s bucket retained + timed!=timeless, canonicalize provenance stamps, insert_article persistence + legacy ALTER heal + idempotent re-init. Neighbors: tests/unit/news/ 41 passed, test_news_phase12 77 passed, integration/test_news_api env-reds (WEB-AUTH 401) reproduced IDENTICAL on clean HEAD (stashed baseline) — pre-existing, unrelated. ruff/mypy clean on news/.
- **Symptom (Verified Lanes 04/06)**: news.db = 233 MB for ~2,120 distinct stories (BoE feed: 11,086 rows / 53 URLs = 209x); 28,237/28,446 rows have |published_at - created_at| < 60 s; executing the adapter's own _parse_dt on real RSS formats reproduced it: 'Mon, 14 Sep 2026 00:05:00 GMT' -> now(). Only the Treasury JSON adapter (ISO-8601 payloads) parsed correctly (0% fabrication).
- **Root Cause (Three Compounding Seams)**: (1) sources/base._parse_dt + ingest/deduplicator._as_dt tried datetime.fromisoformat ONLY; RSS 2.0 pubDate is RFC-822, so every parse failed into a silent `return _utc_now()`; (2) compute_article_hash mixes int(published_at)//60 into the identity, so a fabricated per-poll wall clock minted a fresh article_hash every ~40-min poll — the article_hash UNIQUE guard, is_analyzed_hash and is_junk_hash tombstones CAN NEVER FIRE for those sources (the tombstone tables still grow anyway: 7,861 double-tombstoned hashes); (3) decay/staleness semantics measure INGEST age, not event age, so NewsContext staleness (now-newest>3600s) degenerates to "did the worker run" and the STALE->IGNORE fail-safe cannot fire while alive (observed state census: HIGH_IMPACT 650/704 builds).
- **Fix**: never fabricate, never guess): _parse_dt/_as_dt now parse ISO-8601 AND RFC-822 (email.utils.parsedate_to_datetime incl. named zones) and return None on total failure; canonicalize_item carries published_at_source ('FEED' vs 'INGEST_TIME'); compute_article_hash OMITS the time bucket when no real publish time exists (poll-stable timeless identity) and keeps the 60-s merge bucket when one does; news_articles gains a published_at_source column (CREATE + idempotent ALTER heal; legacy rows backfill 'UNKNOWN' — never retro-guessed); JSONManifest adapter stops stamping now() for missing dates. normalize_datetime() default-when-absent remains the persistence seam (NOT NULL column), but the provenance column now makes the distinction explicit for every consumer.
- **Scope Note**: no historical-DB cleanup in this fix (lane-04 retention/archive plan is a separate workstream); the fix stops the …
- **Tests**: test_bug282_rfc822_and_identity; test_news_api; test_news_phase12; tests/unit/news/ingest/test_bug282_rfc822_and_identity.py
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8644

## BUG-284 - Phase-09 suitability REJECT fired on 1-4 sample families: evidence contract INVERTED (zero evidence passes, ONE loss blocks) - 473/1373 decisions …

- **Root Cause**: the suitability floor had NO sample-count precondition, unlike every other lifecycle threshold in the subsystem (min_samples_evaluating=5 / validated=20 / retire=12 in StrategyEvaluator). Absence-of-evidence was handled at Phase-08; the Phase-09 micro-sample case fell into the full-strength REJECT branch meant for statistically-armed evidence.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug284_suitability_micro_sample_floor.py (7, critical_suite-pinned): n=1 negative-expectancy demotes to WARN with UNCHANGED proposal (RED-before: REJECT+NO_TRADE+conf0.0), n=4 warn-only, n=5 full-strength REJECT preserved (guard-not-weakened pin: action/confidence/blocked_by/counter), positive micro-sample path unchanged, floor constructor-configurable, floor never zero, AND a cross-module contract pin (default floor == StrategyEvaluator.min_samples_evaluating, read from BOTH source signatures — drift of either trips the pin). Neighbors: test_intelligence_phase09 + test_experience_intelligence 128 passed. ruff/mypy clean on gate.py.
- **Symptom**: §4, production ledger): the largest single rejection source after the policy/confidence gates was the soft suitability gate, driven by micro-samples. _suitability_score starts 0.5, clamps expectancy contribution to -0.30, adds -0.10 for n below 5, -0.20 for DEGRADED; min_suitability_to_qualify=0.40. A family with ONE closed losing trade (expectancy -1R, n=1) scores ~0.15 (below 0.40) -> REJECT -> NO_TRADE + confidence 0.0. But ZERO evidence routes to Phase-08 INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE which PASSES the proposal bit-identical. So a strategy was punished harder at n=1-2 than at n=0, and the rejections emit NOT_DISPATCHED outcomes that never count as samples -> the family could not accumulate the 5+ samples needed to escape the penalty it was rejected for. Self-limiting trap ("lost twice -> stop trading"), beyond the designed risk gates (3-loss 1h freeze / survival mode are separate).
- **Fix**: one seam): PreTradeIntelligenceGate gains min_samples_to_reject (default 5 == StrategyEvaluator.min_samples_evaluating, the same "evidence exists" basis this subsystem already uses; constructor-clamped to at-least 1). When suitability is below the qualify floor BUT retrieved_sample_count is below the floor, the verdict DEMOTES to WARN: the proposal passes UNCHANGED (WARN never rewrites action/confidence), with reason MICRO_SAMPLE_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE (n=X) so the funnel still attributes the decision + evidence.samples is stamped. At/above the floor the historical REJECT semantics (NO_TRADE, conf 0.0, blocked_by SUITABILITY_GATE, gate_reject counter) are preserved verbatim. This is NOT a blanket …
- **Note**: deliberate non-changes): (a) Phase-08 experience DEGRADED/PENALIZE (x0.70 confidence) untouched — that is a real …
- **Tests**: test_bug284_suitability_micro_sample_floor; test_experience_intelligence; test_intelligence_phase09; tests/unit/test_bug284_suitability_micro_sample_floor.py
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8659

## BUG-285 - Corrupted-price boundaries failed OPEN: TickData accepted +/-inf (pydantic gt/ge pass inf: ask=inf -> spread inf, both-inf -> spread NaN), BarData …

- **Root Cause**: the price contract was expressed as ORDERING constraints (gt/ge) plus an out-of-band report, not as a FINESS/GEOMETRY contract; corrupted price is on the wave's MUST-FAIL-CLOSED list, so acceptance at the boundary is the defect, not downstream damage potential.
- **Fix**: (1) TickData bid/ask/last/volume gain allow_inf_nan=False (explicit finite contract; ordering validators unchanged). (2) BarData gains a non-finite price field-validator (the canonical series object refuses NaN/inf at construction; the forming-bar sites inside BarAggregator process only finite TickData mids now, so no internal writer changes). (3) get_historical_bars now DROPS structurally-invalid broker rows at the mapping boundary (non-finite, min(open,close)<=0, high<low or outside [low,high], low above open/close) and logs ONE loud HISTORY_INVALID_DROPPED error line per call with received/dropped/kept counts - report-only became enforce-where-scale-is-known: geometry+plausibility live at the broker reader (only it knows the symbol scale), finiteness lives at the model (fits every internal BarData writer incl. synthetic/replay builders which all construct finite bars). The model-level OHLC geometry validator was PROBED and deliberately NOT kept: existing behavior-level fixtures (chart resync: high=101/close=101.01 sweep rows) construct geometrically-loose synthetic bars, and weakening real coverage by rewriting them is not justified when the reader-level check covers the production class. (4) News now() fabrication was BUG-282's half of the same boundary audit; paper-state/gateway-payload gaps (L11-4/5/6) stay open with this wave's follow-up list.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_domain_models.py BUG-285 block (5: inf ask, inf/inf, inf last/volume, NaN pin, finite still accepted incl. spread math) + NEW tests/unit/test_bug285_price_boundaries_fail_closed.py (4: BarData inf/-inf/NaN on all four price fields RED-before-silent, finite constructs, get_historical_bars drops 4 malformed of 6 rows keeping the two good ones + loud-drop shape, None-field skip intact) - both files now critical_suite-pinned (test_domain_models was NOT registered before; it is the TickData/TradeProposal contract owner, gap closed). Neighbors: test_runtime_bars_normalize, test_chart_resync reseed pair (behavioral, still green; the 3 chart-history route tests + test_htf_warmup_gate 9/11/12/13 reproduce IDENTICALLY on clean HEAD - pre-existing env/brittle reds, disclosed not hidden), test_bug232/234, e2e smoke chain green. ruff/mypy clean on touched files.
- **Surface**: probes VERIFIED at 0c855019): TickData(bid=1.0,ask=inf) ACCEPTED -> spread_points=inf; (inf,inf) ACCEPTED -> spread …
- **Tests**: test_bug232; test_bug285_price_boundaries_fail_closed; test_chart_resync; test_domain_models; test_htf_warmup_gate; test_runtime_bars_normalize; tests/unit/test_bug285_price_boundaries_fail_closed.py; tests/unit/test_domain_models.py

## BUG-288 - Audit worker ADOPTS a rebound queue: the "deterministic" BUG-140 stall test is a scheduling coin flip

- **Symptom**: Py Tests (windows-latest) FAILED at b1fe0137 with test_flush_is_bounded_when_worker_stalled - "assert True is False"; the same sha was green 4 minutes earlier at 50a66a71 (same tree, different roll) - a real flake, not a behavior change (the report-only commit touched no code).
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_outcome_flush_race_bug140.py +2 (6 total, file already critical_suite-gated at :42): test_worker_never_adopts_a_rebound_queue_bug288 (deterministic adoption probe - stalls the worker's _connect_sqlite by thread name, swaps + parks an item, releases, asserts the poisoned item stays unconsumed forever) + test_bug288_handshake_source_pins (class guard: wait-after-start ordering, capture-before-publish ordering; a silently-reverted handshake fails CI). RED-BEFORE at the b1fe0137 worktree 2/2, GREEN 6/6 at fix; flake battery 10x serial green; neighbors 121 passed (bug285 drain/wiring, flush-contract, dl-retention, perf-r1, obs-trace x2, bug276, seeder, provenance, runtime-safety, boot-trust, disk-safety, log-hygiene, perf-report-intel, packaged-db). mypy/ruff/format clean.
- **Root Cause (Race Window Verified)**: _start_background_worker returns the instant Thread.start() reports aliveness, but the writer only bound its queue at "q = self._queue" AFTER the OS scheduled the thread AND (pre-fix ordering) the sqlite connect returned. test_flush_is_bounded_when_worker_stalled assumes "the worker captured the ORIGINAL queue at startup": it swaps repo._queue to a fresh queue and parks an item expecting NO consumer. On a slow runner the not-yet-bound worker wakes LATE, captures the SWAPPED queue, drains + task_done()s the item - flush() correctly returns True (drained) and the stall premise evaporates. Deterministic reproduction on this host: gate the worker body, swap + put, release -> item consumed, flush() True (scratch probe, adoption proven). The class is general: ANY self._queue rebinding within the window silently changes which object the writer consumes (queue-depth pins, saturation fixtures, close()/join teardown all read the instance attr).
- **Fix**: INV-001-safe - the tick path never constructs the repo hot): _start_background_worker now passes a threading.Event into the worker; the worker captures q = self._queue FIRST, then ready.set(); the constructor waits (ready, _WORKER_READY_TIMEOUT_SEC=5.0) and logs "AUDIT WORKER CAPTURE NOT CONFIRMED" instead of bricking on a pathological thread. "repo published" now implies "writer bound to self._queue", which is the contract the whole BUG-140 test family already assumed.
- **Tests**: test_bug288_handshake_source_pins; test_flush_is_bounded_when_worker_stalled; test_outcome_flush_race_bug140; test_worker_never_adopts_a_rebound_queue_bug288; tests/unit/test_outcome_flush_race_bug140.py
- **Invariants**: INV-001
- **Commits**: 50a66a71, b1fe0137
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8683

## BUG-289 - telegram_notify CLI rejected the call shapes the CI workflows actually emit: OS-leg and JS-lane finish notifications NEVER delivered

- **Root Cause**: --os was declared on the GLOBAL argparse parser, but the workflow invocation puts the subcommand FIRST and --os AFTER it - argparse routes post-subcommand flags to the SUBPARSER, which never knew --os. The workflow's call shape and the script's grammar diverged at authoring time (tests-os.yml:184 vs telegram_notify main()), and no test exercised the CLI the way CI calls it.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug289_telegram_notify_cli.py (4, critical_suite-pinned): main() driven with the EXACT workflow argv shapes (os-finished --os ..., js-finished, run-finished unchanged, unknown-subcommand still rejected). RED-BEFORE 2/4 at b1fe0137 (both workflow shapes), GREEN 4/4. Telegram unconfigured in tests -> advisory TELEGRAM_CONFIG_ERROR payload, zero network; the pin is that parsing reaches the reporter with the right lane.
- **Symptom (Log-Verified)**: the failed windows job ends with "telegram_notify.py: error: unrecognized arguments: --os windows-latest" (every OS-matrix leg, every run). js-tests.yml:82 calls a js-finished subcommand that does not exist -> usage error. Both steps are || true + continue-on-error, so nothing ever surfaced: the Telegram CI channel has only EVER received the main CI run-finished (which passes no trailing flags).
- **Fix**: _notify.py; .github/workflows/* is outside this job's edit scope, so the script adapts to the shipped call shape): --os declared ON the os-finished subparser (value forwarded to notify_run_finished(os_name=...)); js-finished added as a real subcommand (completion payload tagged 'js'). Global-flag-before-subcommand invocations keep working (argparse default).
- **Acceptance Partial**: #209 CI, second cause disclosed): the js lane now PARSES (no more usage error) but still dies there for an INDEPENDENT pre-existing reason — js-tests.yml installs no Python deps, so `from nexus_scalp.observability...` raises ModuleNotFoundError: structlog (job 104297888448 log 07:49:19Z; hid behind the usage error before, same || true cover). That half needs a workflow edit (add the python setup or drop the step) = OPEN-OPERATOR. tests-os.yml legs install the full env, so os-finished --os is fully effective there (acceptance = next windows/mac leg shows no 'unrecognized arguments').
- **Test-Shape Lesson**: fixed in 25afc95e): the first pin asserted the unconfigured-Telegram payload, but CI injects TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN/CHAT_ID into EVERY job -> the reporter attempted a real send (SEND_FAILED red). CLI grammar pins must spy the DISPATCH seam (class-level monkeypatch of notify_run_finished) and assert the os_name tag, never depend on ambient config.
- **Tests**: test_bug289_telegram_notify_cli; tests/unit/test_bug289_telegram_notify_cli.py
- **Commits**: 25afc95e, b1fe0137
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8689

## BUG-290 - Perf-wave R7+R9: the engine's in-process guard maps grew unbounded for the process life (`_processed_orders` 50k+/day never pruned …

- **Symptom**: long-lived client memory growth class, P3-but-real on 24/7 paper/live boxes: `OrderLifecycleManager._processed_orders` (order_manager.py:510 at 2e53fd04) receives one string->bool entry PER dispatch attempt (dispatch.py:144/512/586) and was grep-proven to have ZERO prune/eviction sites in src/; `_ai_flip_warn_times` (:513) docstring claimed per-ticket cleanup, but `_cleanup_ticket_state` never popped it (proven: no `_ai_flip_warn_times.pop` exists at HEAD) — same "shipped comment, absent code" class as BUG-285's write-only overflow.

## BUG-293 - Lane-11 input-validation fail-open boundaries: corrupt paper_state.json was PARTIALLY APPLIED before failing, and gateway order payloads silently …

- **Severity**: P2 (L11-4: PAPER/SHADOW blast radius — no live capital — but paper experience is training evidence and a half-applied book poisons it; L11-6: gateway sits in front of the LIVE broker order surface, HMAC-authenticated callers could still execute a default-sized order from a payload that lost its volume, and NaN/inf/negative values reached `mt5.order_send` untouched) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/adapters/paper/paper_adapter.py::_load_state` (lane-11 doc §10, GAP L11-4) and `src/nexus_scalp/gateway/server.py::_handle_action` order actions SEND_ORDER / EXECUTE_MARKET_ORDER / PLACE_PENDING_ORDER (§2, GAP L11-6). Evidence: docs/audit/wave_20260914/11_input_validation.md VERIFIED probes.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug293_failopen_boundaries.py (18, critical_suite-pinned): 7 parametrized corrupt paper-state variants (incl. the exact lane-11 probe payload) each asserting engine-state-unchanged field-for-field + exactly one class-named warning via logger probe (BUG-112/118 discipline, never caplog), truncated-JSON + array-payload variants, connect()-never-booted-on-half-state pin, healthy round-trip (test_paper_persistence style), deliberate negative-balance-reload policy pin; gateway: unit pins that the 0.01 default is UNREACHABLE (RED-BEFORE comment), 12 malformed market payloads rejected pre-dispatch, full-HTTP-shape FAILED+reasons with `assert_not_called` on both dispatch seams, valid market payload (price=0 sentinel legal) forwarded verbatim, legacy gt=0 contract.
- **Red-Before**: se bae2dc19 in this worktree, VERIFIED): (a) `_load_state` with balance=-99999/equity=NaN/_ticket_counter="notanint" returned False yet left balance=-99999.0 + equity=nan APPLIED (tickets unchanged, positions dropped) — `connect()` swallows the False and trades on it; (b) `EXECUTE_MARKET_ORDER` with no volume dispatched `volume=0.01`; `volume=inf/price=NaN` dispatched untouched; empty symbol dispatched.
- **Root Cause (L11-4)**: sequential field mutation inside one try — corruption at a late line never rolled back the early ones; no numeric validation (neg balance/NaN equity pass float()); `Position.model_validate` failures `except: continue` (silent book shrink). (L11-6): payload consumed as raw dict with `or` defaults; the market path never got the structural gate the pending path has (`_validate_pending_request`), and defaults pre-empted even that.
- **Fix (L11-4)**: `_load_state` is now candidate-then-swap — `_build_state_candidate` parses+validates the ENTIRE payload (balance/equity finite + non-negative, ticket counter + closed tickets non-negative ints, EVERY position row must validate — a bad row rejects the whole file, never a silent drop) into a detached dict; engine fields are assigned only after full success. On rejection: state untouched, exactly ONE loud structured WARNING `PAPER STATE LOAD REJECTED` with `corruption_class` naming the defect (balance_negative / equity_not_finite / unreadable_or_invalid_json / payload_not_object / position_row_invalid[i] / ...), file left in place for the next healthy persist, never …

## BUG-297 - Perf-wave R5 (the lane-10 §3.1 item-1 twin of BUG-292): the evaluator's derived-registry reads opened a RAW per-call sqlite3.connect on the pre-trade …

- **Fix**: evaluator-owned reuse seam `_registry_conn(timeout)` (contextmanager) + `_open_conn` + `close()`: while the repository runs, ONE handle per (thread, busy-timeout) tier is cached in `threading.local()` — 5.0 read tier, 10.0 write tier — and REUSED by every later registry access (tick-loop tier-3 cost drops to one connect EVER per thread per tier); handles open THROUGH `_connect_sqlite` (URI contract honored, spy-pinned), with a raw-connect fallback ONLY for duck-typed test stubs (one module connect site, AST-pinned). Per-thread caching keeps SQLite's default same-thread guard intact — NO `check_same_thread=False`, no shared-handle lock (pinned), WAL makes concurrent readers on distinct handles safe. Cache key = (repo id, db path): a repository swap or path re-resolution drops stale handles instead of serving the wrong DB (pinned, test_i). Keepalive: any statement failure evicts the cached handle and re-raises INTO THE CALLER'S EXISTING except path — return shapes byte-identical to pre-fix (None / [] / logged clear-failure; registry I/O still never raises into the tick loop, test_c/c2). Closed repository (`_running` False — teardown/tests closing the DB under a live evaluator): the seam releases cached handles (Windows file-lock contract, tmp-DB cleanup — test_c3 even unlinks the file after delegation proves no leak) and behaves EXACTLY pre-fix: one-shot connect per call, closed on every exit. Teardown wiring: LiveEngine shutdown calls `experience_evaluator.close()` before `audit.close()`; `persist_score`-shaped users can close deterministically per thread.
- **Regression**: tests/unit/test_bug297_evaluator_hot_path_reads.py (NEW, critical_suite-pinned): a) stats correct through the REAL ledger write API + async persist queue; b) connect-count CONSTANT over 10 lookups + listing + two clear tiers (RED-BEFORE EXECUTED twice: verbatim pre-fix body = 10 connects/10 lookups; full battery against origin/main evaluator = 13 of 17 sites red, growth-per-call exactly as lane-10 described); c) degradation parity — poisoned path (None/[]/no raise), dead-handle eviction + reopen, closed-repo one-shot delegation + file deletable; d) source pins — no sqlite3.connect/_connect_sqlite inside the three former readers (BUG-285/292 class-guard lesson), exactly ONE module connect site (the stub fallback inside `_open_conn`), no check_same_thread/locking escape; e) production opens route through the repository seam per tier then stop; f) END-TO-END TIER-3: six budget-starved `engine._get_score` calls (real retriever context, real family id, TTL cache dropped, `_last_inline_refresh` poisoned) read …
- **Tests**: test_accounting_core; test_bug284_suitability_micro_sample_floor; test_bug297_e; test_bug297_evaluator_hot_path_reads; test_c; test_c3; test_complete_evidence_loop_0_to_100; test_end_to_end_closed_loop_bug140; test_experience_execution_boundary; test_experience_intelligence; test_experience_provenance_contract; test_i; test_intelligence_phase09; test_obs_trace_chain …

## ADDENDUM — test-file relocation ledger (2026-09-15, NSE-Swarm testrecon wave 2)

Historical entries above cite test files folded into their authoritative suites
during the wave-2 forensic reduction. Name-overlap verification: 0 orphan tests;
supersets exact (87+21=108 into test_forensics.py; 66+26=92 into
test_incidents.py). Old citations remain valid as narratives; the live tests now
live at:

| cited file (historical) | now lives in |
|---|---|
| tests/unit/test_forensic_monitoring_task11.py | tests/unit/test_forensics.py (T1) |
| tests/unit/test_forensic_incident_center_task.py | tests/unit/test_forensics.py (T1) |
| tests/unit/test_incident_response_task12.py | tests/unit/test_incidents.py (T1) |
| tests/unit/test_incident_runtime_task13.py | tests/unit/test_incidents.py (T1) |
| tests/unit/test_dead_letter_store_split.py | store-behavior contracts restored in tests/unit/test_recon_dead_letter_store_contracts.py (T1); the 6 facade-delegation mirror asserts intentionally not restored (wiring-shape per reduction brief §12; facade behavior covered by test_recon_batch_atomicity_real_failure.py + test_runtime_safety_mission.py) |

### Second-pass audit correction (2026-09-16, independent re-verification of the reductions)

The first pass classified all 7 `test_repo_*` tests as delegation mirrors. That was
accurate for 5 of them, but TWO assert observable, incident-bearing behavior of the
PUBLIC `AuditRepository` surface and had NO surviving home anywhere in `tests/`:

| contract | why it is not a mirror | now pinned in |
|---|---|---|
| `test_repo_schema_created_at_setup_zero_migration` | pins that a *constructed repository already carries* `audit_dead_letter` (DDL wired into setup, not deferred to first write) with the 10-column incident-ledger contract. The surviving `test_store_create_table_is_idempotent_verbatim_schema` pins `create_table()` on a **bare store**, which is a different seam: a deferred-DDL regression drops the FIRST dead-letter of a fresh install — the incident nobody would ever see. | `test_recon_dead_letter_store_contracts.py` (T1) |
| `test_repo_batch_recovery_counter_contract_still_holds` | pins facade/store counter AGREEMENT through the public `record_dead_letter` call site the batch-recovery worker uses (`repo.audit_dead_letter_rows == repo.dead_letter_store.audit_dead_letter_rows`) plus the batch-recovery `payload_note` surviving to the rows. `test_recon_batch_atomicity_real_failure.py` does NOT touch `audit_dead_letter_rows` at all; a split-brain counter makes the recovery worker believe rows were salvaged that were never recorded. | `test_recon_dead_letter_store_contracts.py` (T1) |

Both restored pins were MUTATION-PROVEN (executed, not asserted): forcing
`audit_dead_letter_rows` to a stale constant → RED; skipping
`dead_letter_store.create_table(conn)` at repository setup → RED; pristine → GREEN.
Survivor file is now 9 tests (was 7), all GREEN, still critical_suite-pinned.

Deletion-protection ledger: docs/testing/test_protection.json. Full evidence:

It was NOT adopted everywhere: `DirectMT5Adapter` built its default history/tick request windows from the raw constant (mt5_adapter.py:610/617 get_history_orders, 656/663 get_history_deals, 794/795 get_tick_history), and the LIVE directional-entry guard evaluated the maintenance window from the raw constant (execution/lifecycle/dispatch.py:326). An operator setting the env var got epoch OUTPUT conversion at the configured offset but INPUT request windows at 180min — a net skew equal to the difference on every reconcile-baseline history read — and, worse on the money path, an entry guard evaluated on a DIFFERENT clock than the rest of the timebase. Same class as BUG-303: a configurable seam with a bypass consumer.

## BUG-306 - Windows CI critical suite breakages: official_install._sync_file EBADF on "rb" fsync and test_model_studio.py charmap UnicodeDecodeError on …

- **Fix**: (1) `official_install._sync_file`: use `"r+b"` on Windows (`os.name == "nt"`) so the CRT file descriptor has write access, and wrap in `try ... except OSError: pass` to ensure filesystem sync limitations never abort an install transaction. (2) `tests/unit/test_model_studio.py`: specify `encoding="utf-8"` in all `.read_text()` calls.
- **Regression**: `tests/unit/test_bug306_windows_ci_compat.py` (NEW, critical_suite-pinned, stdlib+repo only): - `test_sync_file_normal_file`: validates `_sync_file` on real payload. - `test_sync_file_windows_mode`: validates that when `os.name == "nt"`, `_sync_file` opens with `"r+b"`. - `test_sync_file_handles_ebadf_gracefully`: simulates CRT EBADF `_commit` failure, confirms graceful handling. - `test_sync_file_handles_missing_file_gracefully`: confirms inaccessible/missing paths don't crash caller. - `test_web_ui_assets_utf8_decoding_charmap_regression`: proves `Web/index.html` crashes `cp1252` decode and passes `utf-8` decode. - `test_model_studio_test_file_ast_read_text_encoding_guard`: AST scanner enforcing all `.read_text()` calls in `test_model_studio.py` declare explicit encoding. All 6 new tests + 19 model_studio tests + 17 model_provisioning tests passed (42/42 GREEN). Manifest 202 entries, 0 missing. repo-wide ruff check/format clean; mypy clean.
- **Symptom**: 463 / job 105461019562 `Py Tests (windows-latest)` on main push b361357c): (1) `tests/unit/test_bug298_model_provisioning.py::test_official_path_a_end_to_end` failed with `OSError: [Errno 9] Bad file descriptor`. On Windows, Python's `os.fsync(fd)` delegates to CRT `_commit(fd)`. Microsoft CRT `_commit` documentation specifies: "If fd is an invalid file descriptor, or is open for read only, ... errno is set to EBADF." In `src/nexus_scalp/model_provisioning/official_install.py:225`, `_sync_file` opened files with `with path.open("rb") as stream: os.fsync(stream.fileno())`. Read-only descriptor triggered EBADF under Windows CRT `_commit`. (2) `tests/unit/test_model_studio.py::test_web_ui_assets_exist` failed with `UnicodeDecodeError: 'charmap' codec can't decode byte 0x81 in position 17444: character maps to <undefined>`. On Windows runners, `Path.read_text()` defaults to `cp1252`/`charmap`. `Web/index.html` contains multi-byte UTF-8 sequences that crash cp1252 decode when opened without explicit `encoding="utf-8"`.
- **Tests**: test_bug298_model_provisioning; test_bug306_windows_ci_compat; test_model_studio; test_model_studio_test_file_ast_read_text_encoding_guard; test_official_path_a_end_to_end; test_sync_file_handles_ebadf_gracefully; test_sync_file_handles_missing_file_gracefully; test_sync_file_normal_file; test_sync_file_windows_mode; test_web_ui_assets_exist; test_web_ui_assets_utf8_decoding_charmap_regression …
- **Files**: nexus_scalp/model_provisioning/official_install.py; src/nexus_scalp/model_provisioning/official_install.py
- **Commits**: b361357c
- **Orig**: agents/bugs_archive.md:8830

## BUG-307B - PR #347 / PR #348 / main CI pytest red: TWO distinct schedule-dependent flakes, both root-caused and fixed (2026-09-22, operator verification superseding the first AGENT-QA triage)
- SYMPTOM (VERIFIED): `Code Quality & Tests` failed identically on both open PRs and on `main` itself (run 35687632946, HEAD 9279f1ea, `CHECK pytest rc=1`). On PR #347 head feeb3300 (run 35690139026) and PR #348 head 3c42b087 (run 35688949924) the failure was `test_replay_toggle_guard.py::test_replay_toggle_requires_auth` — `assert 200 == 401`. On main the failing tests were a DIFFERENT pair from `test_mt5_diag_throttle.py`. The failing test changed per run, which is the signature of an xdist-schedule-dependent harness fault, not a code fault in either PR.
- FLAKE 1 (auth probe, FIXED): two test modules wrote the WEB-AUTH opt-out as a bare `os.environ["NSE_WEB_AUTH_DISABLE"] = "1"` with NO restore — `tests/unit/test_model_studio_dataset_order.py` (test body + setup_method) and `tests/unit/test_position_adviser_path_containment.py` (module-scoped autouse fixture). `create_app()` reads the env var at construction time, so every later test on that worker silently had auth OFF and the 401 contract probe saw 200. Fixed with scoped pins (monkeypatch.setenv for function scope; save/restore yield fixture for module scope).
- FLAKE 2 (throttle log capture, FIXED — this is the one the FIRST record described, but with the wrong mechanism): the failing `test_mt5_diag_throttle.py` probes attach a handler to `logging.getLogger("test.mt5.storm")` and assert record counts. `src/nexus_scalp/cli/db_commands.py:_emit` called `logging.disable(logging.CRITICAL)` to keep `--json` output clean and NEVER restored it. `logging.disable` is process-global, so any in-process CLI invocation of `db ... --json` (e.g. `tests/unit/test_bug296_repair_model.py::test_db_migrate_succeeds_without_artifacts_dir`) left every WARNING record silenced for the rest of the worker's life. The probe then read zero records. The structlog `_FixedFindCallerLogger` / `LoggerFactory` theory in the first record is WRONG: the global logger class is irrelevant here, and re-creating a logger object cannot detach a handler. Fixed by wrapping the disable in a save/restore contextmanager so the suppression covers only the JSON print.
- REPRODUCTION RECIPES (both deterministic, torch-CPU): (1) `pytest tests/unit/test_model_studio_dataset_order.py tests/unit/test_position_adviser_path_containment.py tests/unit/test_replay_toggle_guard.py -n 3 --dist loadgroup` -> `assert 200 == 401` plus the `[WEB-AUTH] DISABLED` log line; (2) `pytest tests/unit/test_bug296_repair_model.py::test_db_migrate_succeeds_without_artifacts_dir tests/unit/test_mt5_diag_throttle.py::test_first_failure_always_logs_warning` -> the second test fails. Both pass after the fixes. Neither reproduces serially or on file subsets, which is why the first triage's pairwise bisection found nothing.
- OWNERSHIP NOTE: the first record said the harness fix belonged to the AGENT-QA stream and was deliberately not executed. Both fixes landed on PR #347 because that is where the red was blocking the queue; flake 2 is a one-line src/ fix (scoped suppression), and flake 1 is two-file test hygiene. Neither weakens any contract — the auth opt-out still applies exactly where needed, and --json output is still silent during the emission.
## BUG-307 - 70D shadow reported PHANTOM Champion-vs-Shadow disagreements for ticks where NO shadow inference ran: the neutral placeholder …

- **Severity**: P2 (observability truthfulness; no money path involved — the shadow layer is INV-018 observational and never trades) | STATUS: FIXED
- **Surface**: `src/nexus_scalp/shadow/shadow70/runtime.py` (both invalid branches of observe() + the classify call), `src/nexus_scalp/shadow/shadow70/models.py` (Shadow70Observation / DisagreementClass), `src/nexus_scalp/shadow/shadow70/store.py` (disagreement_counts + list_observations), `src/nexus_scalp/web/model_governance_routes.py` (recent_observations projection + `_shadow70_verdict`), `frontend/src/pages/ML/MLPage.tsx` + `frontend/src/types/domain.ts` (UI verdict cell), `artifacts/audit.db` (2 historical smoke_24 rows relabeled).
- **Root Cause**: `observe()` builds its invalid-path observation with the module-level neutral defaults `shadow_action="NO_TRADE"` / `shadow_confidence=0.0` and then calls `classify_disagreement(champion_action, shadow_action, ...)` UNCONDITIONALLY. With `champion=BUY_MARKET` and the placeholder `NO_TRADE`, the pure classifier correctly returns `CHAMPION_BUYS_SHADOW_NO_TRADE` — and the older code path collapsed to `NO_TRADE_DISAGREEMENT` for the not-READY / vector-rejected branches. So a row that never ran a model got a real disagreement label. Three follow-on effects compounded it: (1) `runtime.summary()["disagreements"]` counted those rows, so the Shadow panel's disagreement total included ticks with no comparison; (2) `store.disagreement_counts()` (CHG-0046 D9 had already moved it to `valid=1`) still counted a valid-but-timed-out row, and `list_observations(disagreement_only=True)` filtered on `agreement=0` alone, so the non-compared rows reached the spec-30 disagreement table; (3) the rows were stamped `sample_source="LIVE"` even though they are `smoke_24` fixtures from a synthetic smoke run, which is what made them indistinguishable from live evidence in the UI.
- **Symptom (Reported By The Operator)**: the "Recent 70D shadow observations" table showed rows like `BUY_MARKET | NO_TRADE | 0.50 / 0.00 | NO_TRADE_DISAGREEMENT | UNKNOWN | NORMAL | PENDING`, with `sample_source=LIVE`, while the shadow runtime was truthfully IDLE. Reading the row, the shadow appeared to be actively refusing the champion's BUY on live ticks. In reality NO 70D model was attached (NO_VALIDATED_CANDIDATE, the documented state since docs/70D_CURRENT_STATE_RECONCILIATION.md) and no shadow inference ran for either row.
- **Fix** (fail-loud, no evidence deleted): (1) NEW `DisagreementClass.NOT_COMPARED` — distinct string, not an alias, so persisted old rows stay readable and the histogram separates them; (2) both invalid branches of `observe()` stamp NOT_COMPARED, and the classify call is now guarded by `valid` — a real class is only computed when an inference actually ran; (3) NEW `Shadow70Observation.compared` property (`valid and error_code == ""`) — the single truthfulness primitive; note it is NOT the same as `valid`, because a valid row can still carry `SHADOW_INFERENCE_TIMEOUT`; (4) `summary()` reports `not_compared` separately and no longer inflates `disagreements`; (5) `store.disagreement_counts()` defaults to `valid=1 AND error_code=''`, and `list_observations(disagreement_only=True)` uses the same condition — the two historical rows are still returned by `list_observations()` (never deleted); (6) the `/api/models/shadow70/summary` projection carries `disagreement = error_code` for non-compared rows via the new `_shadow70_verdict()` helper, plus `valid` / `error_code` / `sample_source` / `confidence_delta` so the UI can distinguish; (7) the UI cell renders a non-compared verdict in the error color with a tooltip stating no shadow inference ran — it never reads as a trade decision; (8) the two `smoke_24` rows in `artifacts/audit.db` were relabeled `sample_source='SMOKE'` (they were never live ticks).
- **Evidence** (executed, not described): reproduced at HEAD with a bare `Shadow70Runtime()` (IDLE, no candidate) — pre-fix `disagreement=NO_TRADE_DISAGREEMENT`, `summary.disagreements=1`; post-fix `disagreement=NOT_COMPARED`, `summary.disagreements=0` + `not_compared=1`. Battery: test_bug278_shadow70_not_compared 12, test_shadow70_runtime 8, test_shadow70_safety, test_shadow70_health_drift, test_shadow_hardening_chg0046 18, test_shadow_replay_evidence_chg0047, test_shadow_phase11, test_bug276_unique_constraint_shadow, test_live_shadow_outcome_resolution 20, test_schema_70d_reconciliation (2 env skips: champion artifact + real 70D dataset absent on this host), test_shadow70_news_family. `frontend/` tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit = 0 errors. No goldens weakened; no contract changed — only the label on rows that never compared.
- **Residual** (open, operator): the 70D candidate gap is UNCHANGED — there is still no validated 70D model, so `NOT_COMPARED` is what every live row will honestly read until one is attached and `sample_source=LIVE` rows exist. The disagreement table is only meaningful after TASK-04's A/B/C benchmark produces a real candidate. Also NOT addressed here: the producer of the `smoke_24` rows was not located in the repo (no source writes that snapshot id; it predates the 2026-09 reconciliation and is likely a deleted ad-hoc smoke script) — relabeling the stored rows is the honest fix, but a future smoke run that touches the production audit DB should pass `sample_source="SMOKE"` explicitly.

## BUG-308 - The broker boundary readers sealed the still-forming current bar, so BarAggregator.reseed() anchored the monotonic tick clock a full minute into the FUTURE and dropped every live tick

- **Severity**: P0 (live market-data feed death; no trade could ever be evaluated) | STATUS: FIXED (5d2ed0f4)
- **Symptom** (live log, 2026-09-21 18:52-18:53 UTC): a continuous flood of `warning Out-of-order tick dropped last_accepted=2026-09-21T18:53:00+00:00 tick_ts=2026-09-21T18:52:52+00:00` while the wall clock was still INSIDE minute 18:52, i.e. the acceptance floor was ~8s ahead of real time. The feed starved from a single reseed and only `NO_TRADE / NO_CANDIDATE_RANGING_MEAN_REVERSION` could be produced. The coincident `POST http://178.105.20.69:11434 503` lines are an unrelated external AI/research host outage, not the cause.
- **Root cause**: `DirectMT5Adapter.get_historical_bars`, `RemoteMT5GatewayAdapter.get_historical_bars`, `PaperMT5Adapter.get_historical_bars` and the `/api/chart/history` reseed path all hardcoded `is_complete=True` on EVERY bar returned. MT5 `copy_rates_*` ships the still-forming CURRENT minute in the same payload as its sealed history, so that minute reached `BarAggregator.reseed()` as a completed candle. reseed() anchors the monotonic tick clock at `last_bar.timestamp + 1m`; with the last "completed" bar being the current minute, the anchor landed a full minute in the future and the out-of-order guard correctly dropped every real tick stamped inside it. The guard worked; its input was a lie.
- **Fix** (two layers):
  1. Boundary classification (primary): new `is_current_bar_forming(bar_ts, timeframe, now=None)` in `indicators/resample.py` (floors to the bar open via `bucket_start`, forming while `now < open + timeframe`; unmapped TFs W1/MN1 fail OPEN to complete so history is never dropped). The four boundary readers now emit `is_complete=False` for the bar the market clock is still inside. reseed() already filters incomplete bars, so the anchor stays in the past and the first real tick of the forming minute is accepted.
  2. `BarAggregator.reseed()` defense-in-depth: refuse to point the tick clock into the future — clamp the anchor to the last completed bar own open and log it. Forming-bar timestamp geometry (`next_minute`) is unchanged; only the acceptance floor moves.
- **Contracts preserved**: completed-bar series stays strictly sealed + ascending + unique; the out-of-order guard is untouched (a stale tick is still dropped, pinned by a test); `BarResampler` still skips forming bars; both UI consoles already render `is_complete===false` as "Forming" (Web/app.js, frontend chartPainter).
- **Evidence** (executed): RED-BEFORE reproduced byte-exact against the production log line (anchor +60s in the future, first tick DROPPED); post-fix the anchor stays at the last completed bar and the first tick is accepted (forming bar volume 0->1, close updated). 13/13 new tests; 49/49 tests/integration/test_mt5_adapter_parity.py; test_market_data_integrity_ag13 green.
